# Liber Primus: current research state

## Decision-focused synthesis

**Status, 13 November 2026.** The 56 unresolved LP2 physical pages (p0–p55; p50 has no runic text) contain **12,956 unknown runes** across 2,896 printed words. No unknown LP2 plaintext, creator-authenticated decoding key, complete original-cipher inverse or confirmed continuation destination has been recovered. Most tests below are *conditional exclusions* of specified mechanisms against specified control corpora. They cannot substitute for a plaintext-bearing result.

**Established positive inputs.** Solved p56 supplies an authentic page-local prime-totient cipher control and solved p57 a clear-text GP control. Original p15 has a 16-cell Fibonacci-indexed prime spiral whose exact generation/read order is reproducible, but whose LP2 cipher use is unknown ([source construction](lp2-p15-fibonacci-prime-spiral.md)). Crucially, actual solved LP1 word spellings show single GP runes for **ING** and **IO**: a source-calibrated English encoder recovers 181/181 LP1 word positions and independently all 95 original p57 runes. This changes the meaning of earlier natural-English exclusions based only on inventory-label encoding; both controls must be distinguished ([source-orthography result](lp1-source-orthography.md)).

**Original-source leads, still unselected as decipherment mechanisms.** The pp49–51 **32×8 numerical grid**, repeated visual/red motifs, exact p3/p39 red-triad Atbash outputs `SCY`/`UDP`, source-visible chapter boundaries, the 256-rune terminal p55–57 count, and p5/p55 moth-linked seven-word *proposed crib* each constrain candidate investigations to varying degrees. None supplies an independently authenticated key, operator, consumption clock and reversible decoder for unknown runes. Source-fitted arithmetic coincidences and language correlations remain hypotheses. See [the integrated inversion challenge](../workplace/integrated-original-inversion-challenge.md) for bounded routes and the present missing input.

**Next decisive gate.** Prefer a newly sourced, lossless original-cipher operation whose parameters are fixed by the artifact or authenticated historical instruction *before* scoring the target. Test authentic solved controls, an untouched unknown interval, coherent plaintext and exact forward re-encryption. An invertible cipher with a freely chosen key always round-trips its own output; source-independent key selection and original plaintext evidence are essential. Revisit earlier finite English-control exclusions when the source-calibrated spelling materially changes their premise; avoid repeating already exhausted key sweeps merely to enlarge the negative catalog.

The detailed evidence inventory follows. Each result retains its exact tested scope, report and reproducer; a closed issue or passing synthetic plant alone does not establish an LP2 solution.

**Complete p55 minimum symbol-change budget (12 November 2026).** The corrected physical p55 has a 75-rune body in 16 complete printed words, preceded by one p54 NG carry. Across all **606,663** overlapping same-length GP-native LP1/Shakespeare/Gibran/Emerson control windows, a free unknown global 29-symbol relabeling plus arbitrary transposition can approach a 75-rune inventory within **one** identity change, so this unrestricted edit requirement alone is weak. Retaining all 16 original word boundaries and permitting arbitrary *within-word* transposition, the **exact maximum-weight alphabet-assignment** instead requires at least **23 rune identity changes** for every pinned 75-rune control (and **23** for 76 with the incoming carry). The fully scanned solved-LP1 controls require **27/75** and **28/76**, respectively. A separate exact **43-interior-only** assignment grants arbitrary replacement of all **32 word-exterior runes** and all global alphabet relabelings/within-word permutations, but requires **at least 8 changed interior rune identities** to match any pinned 75-rune native-English control (11 for solved LP1). Perimeter-only transformations therefore cannot recover those candidate native passages. These are finite-corpus minima against optimistically fixed target word partitions, not universal plaintext exclusions, recovered GP text or an authentic source-selected cipher. The two previously tested direct 25-cell/prime-totient p55 transfers remain negative. [Proof, corpus counts, exact plants and SFT](../im-results/p55-wisdom-5x5-original-75-gate.md#exact-minimum-symbol-change-budgets-for-the-complete-original-body).

**Pure 43-interior p55 transposition obstruction (12 November 2026).** The 43 strict interior runes of physical p55 have a fixed-label GP29 one-gram log score **−4.132515** which no position-only permutation can improve. **0/431,420** overlapping same-length Shakespeare/Gibran/Emerson GP-native English control windows score as poorly, using a Shakespeare-trained model and independent heldout texts. Thus every *pure* permutation of these 43 unchanged rune identities is strongly disfavoured as ordinary native-GP English, independent of its key or route. With one secret global output-alphabet permutation, the exact optimized unigram score becomes **−2.987154** and **360/431,420** equivalently optimized controls score as poorly; substitution-plus-transposition remains possible. Across the same three held-out corpora, even allowing **arbitrary rune-position rearrangement**, an output must change **at least 14 of the original 43 rune identities** to reproduce any observed native-GP English 43-rune letter inventory (14 Shakespeare, 15 Gibran, 14 Emerson); this is a finite-corpus minimum, not an all-English theorem. Five of its printed two-rune words (`SB`, `XM`, `WR`, `EOE`, `THOE`) have **zero** strict-interior symbols; they remain ciphertext under *every* interior-only operation, and none appears among **36,150** GP-native two-rune English control words. This independently requires an exterior-altering operation if the proposed 43-interior transformation is intended to recover the entire printed 76-rune page. A source-fixed permutation from the 43 authenticated 2016 signed-message GP-prime-summed word ranks and its inverse are nonlinguistic on the original, despite valid planted/native inverse checks. A further fixed additive key from the same signed message's 43 ASCII-letter or GP-rune word lengths fails on the p55 43-core and the p27 215-rune black body repeated five times; the best p55 score remains below every matched native control, and all p27 variants score worse than raw. Direct zero-/one-based p55-rune-to-word indexing also produces no coherent phrase. These are bounded negative tests, not a solution or proof that the signed message has no semantic/navigation role. No recovered plaintext or historical key. A GP-normalized pure-transposition control also tested Latin Vulgate Luke 1 (204,771 windows), heldout Latin Revelation (2,358), and the TEI body of Old Norse Völsunga saga (104,702): zero windows in each corpus scored as poorly as the fixed 43-rune target; minimum histogram L1 distances were 30, 34 and 30. This excludes only a contiguous excerpt from these specific corpora under the stated normalization, not other languages or symbol-changing ciphers. [Report](../im-results/lp2-signed2016-43word-primerank-p55-original-gate.md) · [Original SFT](../sims/lp2_2016_signed_prime_rank_p55_sft.cjs).

**Previous-ciphertext-state all-secret capacity (13 November 2026).** The original 12,901 within-page adjacent pairs impose an exact row-occupancy ceiling on every **complete**, stationary decoder `P_i=D_{clock,C_(i−1)}(C_i)` with an arbitrary secret 29-output row table at each preceding-cipher rune. Any one native GP29 plaintext rune can occupy at most **714** original positions under one book-wide table, **1,255** under nine independently secret visual-chapter tables, or **1,216** under eight phase tables; each of **88** untouched source-native Shakespeare/Gibran/Emerson control windows needs at least **1,419** occurrences of its most frequent rune. A complete Latin26→GP29 row decoder, even with three freely assigned homophones per row, admits at most **4,232** occurrences of any four Latin letters book-wide, against a minimum **4,482** in all **94** original-layout Latin26 controls. Exact synthetic 12,956-rune GP29/Latin26 plants pass. The exclusion is finite-corpus and assumes a complete plaintext alphabet at every specified public state; additional key state/history, page-secret alphabets, radically different plaintext or external side information remain untested. **Alphabet-support qualification:** these Vulgate passages actually use 22–23 distinct Latin letters and 25–27 distinct GP runes. Even allowing each candidate a secret codebook for *only the distinct letters occurring in that window*, **27/31 raw Latin26** and **30/30 Latin GP29** Vulgate controls still violate the book-wide top-five capacity; the smaller-register chapter/eight-phase case is not excluded by the full-alphabet statement alone. A separately source-pinned **31-window uncontracted Latin26 Vulgate** control (208,680 genuine Latin letters retaining Q/V/K/Z, exact original book SHA witnesses) also excludes every complete **book-global 26→29** row codebook: its minimum top-four / top-five plaintext counts are **5,258 / 6,250** versus original upper bounds **4,232 / 4,759**. An independent **30-window Latin Vulgate native-GP29** control (204,802 source-pinned runes) also violates the book/chapter/eight-phase first-order capacity in every window: its minimum dominant-rune count is **1,467** and top-five count **6,318**. Extending the exact capacity test across all plaintext symbol group sizes and page-local periods 1–20, **none of 88 GP29 windows** satisfy the necessary frequency conditions for any period 1–12 (the first two pass at 13), and **none of 94 Latin26 windows** pass any period 1–8 (the first 14 pass at 9); these are envelope constraints, not discovered clocks or decoders. No original plaintext or key. [Reproducer and theorem](../im-results/lp2-firstorder-cipherstate-alphabet-capacity.md) · [manual CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43413679090).

**Plaintext-history cipher capacity, any secret table (12 November 2026).** The exact overlapping-window theorem rules out direct GP29 native-English decoding by any deterministic, page-static transducer depending on the current plaintext rune and at most seven preceding plaintext runes: a repeated plaintext 11-gram forces a repeated ciphertext four-gram, yet the original has only one such within-page pair and all 89 matched Shakespeare/Gibran/Emerson windows have at least two repeated 11-grams. The original eight-column phase separately fails for every matched control with at most three preceding plaintext runes. **The q=8 boundary is now explicit:** the 12-to-four collision condition admits only two of 89 windows (both Emerson), but both fail exact deterministic input consistency, with 13/14 identical (history,current-rune) inputs requiring different ciphertext outputs. Thus no matched native-GP control fits the tested q=8 deterministic family either. A short 528-rune q=8 secret-permutation plant recovers exactly. The two coarse survivors each use 12,484 distinct page-specific history contexts; a literal sparse table of full 29-symbol permutations would need about 160 KB, while a compact generator remains possible but has no source-selected seed or recurrence. These are finite native-English family exclusions, not an identified historical cipher or recovered plaintext. [Full derivation and SFT](../im-results/lp2-plaintext-history-determinism-collision-gate.md) · [Reproducer](../sims/lp2_plain_history_transducer_collision_sft.cjs).

**Physical table boundaries:** The original 32×8, 256-cell numerical grid spans LP2 physical **pp49–51** (80, 104 and 72 cells respectively; archive scans `66.jpg`–`68.jpg`). Physical **p50 alone holds 104 cells**. Below, “p50 table” is historical shorthand for the complete three-page grid unless a page-local subset is specified. This distinction matters for page ordering, rune offsets and proposed human-visible key instructions.

**Original 256 punctuation/page intervals and all-secret table-link obstruction (13 November 2026).** The unknown text has 175 period marks, 17 transcribed ampersand marks and eight dollar marks; reinstating runeless physical p50 yields 55 physical page changes, hence **256** maximal intervals for the **256** cells of the pp49–51 grid. This retrospective, delimiter-dependent count includes **37 empty slots** and supplies no author-prescribed assignment or key. Each interval length fits a byte (maximum 240); the **37 empty intervals address 33 distinct printed table bytes**, proving that every stationary deterministic interval-to-byte checksum or hash must disagree at **at least 35** of these slots, even with an arbitrary secret function. Direct bytewise XOR of the 256 lengths with the 256 printed bytes gives no readable ASCII text or file signature. [Bounded byte-consumer inverse](../im-results/lp2-original-punctuation-256-slot-grid-gate.md#exact-256-byte-interval-length-consumer-agent18-13-november-2026). Crucially, any independently secret, stationary GP29 alphabet permutation for *each* interval preserves the pooled within-interval coincidence **0.033742684**, far below the **0.058203–0.063635** range of all 45 exact-layout Shakespeare/Gibran/Emerson native-GP controls. One synthetic 256-secret-alphabet native plant round-trips all 12,956 runes. Simple printed/reversed table-byte ±GF29 per-interval shifts also fail (full output IoC **0.034456–0.034508**). This excludes the direct 256-slot fixed-substitution interpretation against those native controls, with **no recovered unknown plaintext or secret key**. [Original-input SFT](../sims/lp2_grid256_punctuation_interval_allsecret_sft.cjs) · [Source and bounded result](../im-results/lp2-original-punctuation-256-slot-grid-gate.md) · [manual Windows CI success](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43390815578).

**Earlier 2014 original onion-2 256-byte key (13 November 2026).** An independently archived contemporaneous IRC bot record and a 2014 walkthrough agree on a **complete 256-byte Cicada-served onion-2 string** (progressively appended 6–7 January 2014), now retained byte-exactly as [source data](../resources/2014-onion2-final-256byte-hex.txt). This is a genuine historical candidate key with the **same byte length** as the printed LP2 pp49–51 table; the manuscript provides no surviving instruction pairing them. Source-ordered/reversed bytewise XOR and modulo256 ± produce **78–98 printable ASCII bytes among 256**, with no recognizable file/message header; exactly **one** byte matches at the same position, and the inventories are different. Treating the onion bytes, table bytes, or their XOR as a repeated 256-step GP29 key over all 12,956 unknown runes, under two signs and separate solved-page literal-F options, gives original-like full IoC **0.0344515–0.0358114**, with **no recovered original plaintext**. These conditional direct-consumer failures leave more complex key derivation open only if independently source-selected. [Historical-key source report](../im-results/lp2-2014-onion2-256bytes-grid-source-gate.md) · [fully reversible CJS SFT](../sims/lp2_2014_onion2_grid256_historical_key_sft.cjs) · [manual Windows CI success](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43392764070).

**Provisional red-fragment Atbash clue (12 November 2026).** Original LP2 p3 and p39 contain the **only two isolated three-rune red spans**, identically placed at page-local offset119 and each bracketed by red 13-dot marks. The already solved LP1 GP29 Atbash (index ↦ 28−index) converts these exact original triples to `SCY` (GP spelling consistent with **SKY**, since GP C covers K) and **`UDP`**. The 2013 creator-signed sky clue was historically solved through **ICMP**, so a new UDP instruction or key is **hypothetical**; no destination, port, payload, unknown-page plaintext or global inverse has been authenticated. [Exact red119 analysis and SFT](../im-results/lp2-p3-p39-red119-atbash-sky-udp.md).

**Numerical radix discriminant (12 November 2026).** With the original pp49–51 256-cell tokens held in printed row-major order, treating conventional base60 bytes as descending coefficients over GF(29) makes the entire 256-slot polynomial (actual GF29 degree **254**, since the first byte203 vanishes mod29) exactly divisible by both the 2013 BASE29-example quadratic `3+26X+24X²` and the p39-red-triad quadratic `27+5X+15X²`. The competing literal base59 alphabet obtained by omitting unused lowercase `f` leaves product remainder `[8,19,1,7]`; reversing coefficient order also fails. This selects **base60 over this base59 alternative for the sourced factor hypothesis**, with no novel plaintext or authenticated authorial polynomial instruction. The dual-factor quotient has **251 significant coefficients** despite its historical 252-slot zero-prefixed serialization; the newly checked **period-251** and prior padded period-252 direct GP29 key interpretations both remain nonlinguistic. Even allowing all **55 independent secret per-page GP29 output alphabets** after the exact period-251 key, the source-ciphertext frequency bounds are only **802** occurrences for any one plaintext rune and **7,151** total among its most frequent 12 (most generous sign), compared with the independently solved native-LP1 proportions that would require about 1,600 and 10,059 at this manuscript length. This is a conditional all-key exclusion of native-English-like output for this quotient architecture, rather than a new decryption ([source-exact test](../im-results/p50-p39-p3-field-remainder.md#direct-polynomial-quotient-as-messagekey--bounded-negative)). Factor choices were retrospective, so independent proof of intentional encoding remains outstanding. [Complete source-exact comparison and SFT](../im-results/p50-p39-p3-field-remainder.md#literal-base60-versus-f-skipping-base59-factor-discriminator-agent-3-12-november-2026).

**Source-visible chapter boundaries and distinct nine-section clock (12 November 2026).** The original Stage11 publication fixes page order p0–p57. An original-image red-heading/preceding-page-red13 rule selects the top-level unknown page chapters **pp0–2, 3–7, 8–14, 15–22, 23–26, 27–32, 33–39, 40–53, 54–55**; pp56 and 57 each have their own red opening. Their exact unsolved GP29 lengths are **[729, 1145, 1729, 1903, 1021, 1433, 1680, 3008, 308]**. This differs from the often-used derivative nine-*text*-section lengths **[729, 1145, 1729, 9, 1894, 1021, 1524, 1589, 3316]**: the original visual cuts include global rune offsets **7960 and 12648**, while the derivative-only cuts are **3612 and 8051**. In particular, p53 closes with red13 and p54 starts a new rubric even though the pp40–55 floral artwork is identical. These source-visible chapter boundaries are authentic *reading-structure candidates*, **not established cipher key resets**; #510 closes the full page/chapter/word/line/red13 clock audit: the original 2014 page list, visual chapters, physical word/line boundaries and red13 marks supply no cipher-state reset; p56's φ(prime) clock is page-local. previous nine-text-reset tests apply only to their exact separate cuts. The direct solved-p56 φ(prime) transfer to the 308-rune p54–55 visual chapter fails (no-skip score **−7.42993** versus raw **−7.36976**; solved p56 **−4.46165**). Exhausting **all 64 literal-F skip masks** at that chapter's six ciphertext F sites yields best quadgram **−7.26565** (maximum IoC **0.03716**); all **8** page-local p55 masks similarly fail (best **−7.03622**). This only retires the directly transferred cipher with either source-visible start clock. [Source-page atlas, source controls and SFT](../im-results/lp2-global-original-visual-section-map.md) · [p54–55 transfer SFT](../sims/p54_55_p56_totient_transfer_sft.cjs). A fixed Q252 chain-step consumer using these original visual chapters was also tested: the nine 28-symbol quotient rows, reduced mod28, act as repeating per-chapter keys on the book-continuous ΔC stream; the 86 zero steps emit EA and do not consume key, while only the key row changes at chapter boundaries. Both signs and both initial-symbol conventions fail (full scores −14.0642 to −14.0795; best 238-rune window −13.0904 versus native minimum −8.5671; wrong-seed controls indistinguishable; 12,956-rune positive plants recover exactly). This retires only this fixed Q252-to-chain-step schedule, not the chain hypothesis or other Q252 uses ([report](../im-results/2013-base29-gf29-grid-factor.md#q252-rows-on-chain-step-differences); [SFT](../sims/p50_q252_chain_step_key_sft.cjs)).

**LP2 printed-word structure (13 November 2026).** The **2,896** exact original whole printed words show nearly random adjacent word-length ordering: nine-class χ² **68.2732**, ordinary against 20,000 exact-inventory shuffles (p **0.332**). All **63** native-GP English/Latin control windows have stronger adjacency even after exact histogram rank-matching; all **630** spelling/tie sensitivity controls agree. Nine visual-chapter exact-inventory shuffles likewise yield ordinary adjacent-length mutual information within eight of nine chapters. Original-chapter histogram-matched native GP English controls show lower MI in p23–26, p33–39 and p40–53 relative to most native windows (nominal lower-tail 0.021/0.028/0.018), but none withstands nine comparisons, and the other chapters overlap native prose. This motivates testing source-selected within-chapter order operations without treating them as established. A possible **362×8** interleaver borrows its eight columns from the separate original **32×8** numeric table, but the manuscript does not actually prescribe 362 rows. The two original natural row/column inverses give χ² **71.7270/70.1399** and are ordinary against 5,000 matched shuffles (p **0.4065**). A full extension now optimizes **all 40,320 eight-column orders**. Its best χ² is **116.1556**, yet **56/100** optimally shuffled controls meet/exceed it (p **0.564**). A **separately Shakespeare-trained** length-adjacency model selects a *different* optimal order on the original (score **−8.16823**); **405/500** fully optimized matched shuffles score as well or better (p **0.810**). The same trained model scores **31/31** independently scrambled, histogram-matched Gibran/Emerson controls above the original, with exact true-key recovery in **16/31**. The preexisting [word-collision equality bound](../im-results/lp2-word-collision-injective-source-gate.md) is stronger against *pure* intact-word permutations; this 8! grammar test additionally covers unknown word-internal length-preserving substitutions. The complete 8! family therefore supplies no supported original word order, genuine key or unknown plaintext in this one-pass intact-word model. [Grammar](../im-results/lp2-wordlength-order-grammar.md) · [consolidated all-order report](../im-results/lp2-original-8column-wholeword-transpose-gate.md) · [exhaustive and planted SFT](../sims/lp2_8column_allperm_wordgrammar_sft.cjs).
**Original p15 spiral and exact 16-column whole-word order (13 November 2026).** The original 4×4 Fibonacci-prime spiral supplies a fixed 16-column order and the 2,896 unknown printed words fit 16×181 exactly. Under a one-pass, length-preserving whole-word reading, the source spiral scores **−45.60199** on a separately Shakespeare-trained adjacent-word-length model versus original **−33.98050**; an **exact Held–Karp optimization over all 16! column orders** reaches only **11.23244**, which **93/96** fully reoptimized original-inventory shuffles meet or exceed (upper-tail p **0.96907**). All **31/31** independent length-matched Gibran/Emerson English transposition plants optimize above the original and invert exactly. This retires the proposed whole-word 16-column direct GP-English readout, with no key or plaintext; it does not constrain changed word boundaries, another language or multi-layer encryption. [Full source and controls](../im-results/lp2-p15-spiral-16column-word-dp-gate.md) · [Exact DP SFT](../sims/lp2_p15_spiral_16column_word_dp_sft.cjs) · [Windows CI pass](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43385702437).

**Source-side-state selection gates (13 November 2026; #723/#724 CLOSED).** The source has **32 cross-page carried words** and the numeral grid **32 rows**, but 30,000 nine-chapter and physical-page-length-preserving complete-word randomizations give mean **35.707** carried words, with **3,842/30,000** containing 32 or fewer (one-sided p **0.128**); this count does not select a row/key assignment. The original 2,896 printed word lengths contain **83 one-rune words (2.87%)**, a fraction broadly comparable with native-GP Gibran/Emerson prose (~3.10%/~3.03%), but strongly below **647.157** expected if their 2,895 separators were placed uniformly among 12,955 rune gaps. This small probability is therefore expected for language-like word lengths and excludes only that unconstrained random-spacing null; a page- and carry-constrained null predicts **641.004** and none of 10,000 controls reaches 83. The fixed word-length histogram nevertheless admits **8,791.876 bits of abstract ordering rank**, without an authentic rule for reading it as a lossless cipher-exception tape. The unknown manuscript has **213 original red GP29 runes in 16 spans**, with visual-chapter counts **[13,44,12,20,16,19,21,59,9]**; no author-selected red-to-event map, consumption clock, or inverse was found. In pp8–14, 12 red runes offer only a hypothetical 58.30-bit free tape versus a 51.97-bit nine-repeat position bound, but the red glyphs are themselves part of the ciphertext and cannot be counted again as independent side information. Both source-selected side-state proposals end at a missing decoder, not recovered plaintext or a universal cipher exclusion. [Word-length report](../im-results/lp2-wordlength-sidecar-marginal-gate.md) · [red-layer decision, Issue #724](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/724) · [word-length decision, Issue #723](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/723).

**LP2 repeated-rune event selection (12–13 November 2026).** The 86 same-page adjacent repeats occupy **85 canonical printed words** (84 with one event, one with two). That 86/85 occupancy is ordinary under a distinct source-preserving 20,000-draw null: keep all 2,896 word identities/lengths and 12,956 rune positions fixed, then relocate the 86 event sites uniformly within either the 55 physical-page strata or 72 page × 13-dot strata, preserving each stratum's event count. The observed **one** within-word collision compares with null means **1.40620±1.14167** and **1.32425±1.12286** (lower-tail p **0.58692/0.61497**); it supplies no evidence for a one-repeat-per-word emitter or meaningful 85-symbol register. Separately, the rune-gap lag-one correlation (+0.38002) remains unusual under the page × 13-dot × delimiter × exact-word-length uniform-site null (p=0.00770). Conditioning on the exact event-bearing word IDs and counts, then randomizing positions only within those words, makes the gap correlation ordinary (20,000 draws: mean +0.37855, SD 0.00227, p=0.25914); the 85 event-bearing word-index gaps themselves have lag-one correlation +0.38545. A whole-word-profile null relocates complete repeat-position patterns among word/page slots matched by physical page, exact word length and eligible-site count: p=0.021449, or p=0.008200 when the original 13-dot fragment is also preserved. This remains a **model-specific residual about which word IDs receive patterns**, not a source-selected predictor, cipher state, or research-wide significance claim. No plaintext, key or generator is recovered. A distinct exploratory Latin26 register comparison remains closer in fixed-corpus word-length KL to the original than both legacy and source-calibrated GP29 controls across four corpora; this is not evidence of Latin plaintext. The proposed K repeat marker is postselected. A correct predecessor-relative rank test (rank of each nonrepeat Cᵢ among the 28 runes other than Cᵢ₋₁) finds all 28 ranks in every visual chapter and 54/55 physical pages. A fixed 25-letter→28-rank map is therefore impossible globally, per chapter, or per page on 54/55 pages. This does not exclude a rank assignment that changes within pages/words or a position-keyed encoder; no source-selected update rule or key is known. The only page-level exception is p55, which uses 24 ranks. Its first rune is a carry-in fragment completing a word begun on p54; the remaining 75 body runes form 16 complete words. The p55-only 25→28 predecessor-rank exception has now been source-gated (#741). Its first 45 runes have 23 distinct relative ranks with maximum frequency three, and the seventh complete word has an exact 13-letter equality shape with eleven distinct symbols. That word pattern has no match in four native English reference corpora, 1,578 thirteen-letter CMU dictionary entries, or 241 thirteen-letter words in eight independently source-pinned Latin Vulgate books. The Vulgate's entire 39,835-token original register has just 23 distinct Latin letters and no K, so it cannot encode p55's 25 distinct required letter classes or the one compulsory literal K marker. None of 500,000 word-length-matched English/Latin native-text plants also matches the original first45 or full75 frequency constraints. The seventh-word equality invariant holds under **every** fixed secret injective 25-letter rank map, even if a new map is chosen independently for every printed word, provided it remains fixed *within* that word. More key permutations or word-boundary resets cannot repair this word-shape mismatch against the checked native dictionaries; the pooled-frequency rejection applies to a page-stationary map. The 24 occupied ordinary ranks alone are unsurprising under an iid uniform 28-rank occupancy null (conditional probability **0.0952473751** for at most24 in 74 nonrepeats). No plaintext or source-selected changing-state key was found. [All-key original-input gate](../im-results/lp2-p55-latin-predecessor-rank-wordshape-gate.md) · [source SFT](../sims/lp2_p55_latin_rank_wordshape_sft.cjs) · [passing Windows CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43413206644). The first-repeat-rune 85-symbol carrier independently fails p56 under every fixed GP29 alphabet bijection (IoC **0.03361344538** versus **0.07002801120** for p56 plaintext); no original instruction selects an 85-to-85 book-code mapping (#717). **Retrospective red-channel filter-ratio match (13 November 2026).** On unsolved LP2 pages p0–55, 13 of the 16 annotated red-rune spans begin with a red dropcap; the three unmarked spans are p7@194, p15@9 and p53@128. The countable ratio 13/16 = 0.8125 was retrospectively compared with the rejection probability s inferred from the ciphertext's repeat deficit; no original instruction maps red dropcaps to a rejection parameter. With q=1/29, this predicts 85.82 repeats across 12,901 within-page transitions (86 observed; MLE s=0.81210, approximate 95% CI 0.772–0.852); flattening page joins predicts 86.18/86 (MLE 0.81288). The match is nondiscriminating: nearby simple fractions 4/5 and 9/11 predict 91.50 and 83.23 repeats, respectively. Direct source-key/seed constructions from the 30-bit red-dot stream and 13-dropcap sequence, tested against Perl 5.14 drand48, Python 2.7 MT, glibc rand and RC4, produced no plaintext path. Treat 13/16 as an unselected numerical match pending an independently sourced red→cipher rule, not a privileged parameter for further seed/key searches. [Report](../im-results/lp2-red-ink-1316-filter-parameter-gate.md) · [reproducer](../sims/lp2_red1316_filter_parameter_sft.cjs).

**Original repeat-site fixed-escape marker gate (12 November 2026).** A strictly lossless GP29→28 predecessor-exclusion channel using **one fixed literal reserved plaintext rune** must map all same-page ciphertext repeats to exactly that plaintext rune. Original pp0–55 have **86** repeats, **23** at printed-word beginnings, **3** beginning two-rune words, excluding all 55 rune-bearing physical-page starts; the resulting one-rune occurrence/location signature is exactly **(86,23,3)**. An original-input [CommonJS SFT](../sims/lp2_zero_repeat_marker_wordstart_sft.cjs) preserves all 2,896 original printed words and exact physical boundaries. In **7,500** independently seeded length-matched GP-native word resamplings over Shakespeare, Gibran, Emerson, Unicode-corrected Old English Beowulf and Latin Vulgate, **zero** reproduce all three original counts for **any** of the 29 possible fixed reserve symbols. Sensitivity matters: under a loose ±10 occurrence and ≥23 word-start condition, Latin **645/1,500** controls do pass (often from too many word starts); under ±10 occurrences and ±5 word starts, Latin has five near matches and Emerson two. The Old English test substitutes authentic length-14 *source rune spans* for exactly three layout slots where its native words have no length-14 examples. Four directly printed original solved-Cicada GP passages offer a distinct authentic cleartext control of **1,096 runes, 250 words, eight EA symbols and zero EA word beginnings**. Complete 12,956-rune reversible plants with fixed markers F, J and EA preserve the exact source event geometry and invert fully. A complementary **all-29 within-word/final-repeat gate** preserves the 2,896-word exact GP lengths and uses the original **63 within-word doublets, 19 word-final doublets** instead of the earlier 86/23/3 signature. Even granting an unknown choice among all 29 fixed GP plaintext tokens, the closest joint matched-length expectation is NG (98.88,53.80) for Shakespeare, TH (78.27,43.16) for Emerson and W (103.35,30.54) for Gibran, versus original (63,19); each author's 1,000 independently sampled controls has **0** best-of-29 joint-discrepancy scores as extreme as the original. This **corroborates** the already bounded single-reserved-token exclusion from a different event geometry; it does not independently authenticate an event mechanism or plaintext. [Consolidated EA/all-rune wordfinal gate](../im-results/lp2_ea_sentinel_wordfinal_source_gate.md) · [all-29 SFT](../sims/lp2_single_reserved_rune_wordshape_sft.cjs). **Position-uniform repeat baseline:** Across 12,901 eligible original positions, 2,873 begin printed words. If 86 repeat events were distributed uniformly independent of position, the expected word-start count is **19.15185** and the original 23 has exact hypergeometric upper-tail **0.190316**. The original 55-page event-count dispersion similarly has seeded conditional-uniform tail **0.300135** in 20,000 runs. Thus the word-start rate is unusual for a single native-English `EA` literal marker but **ordinary for a rare position-driven event process**; this provides a materially distinct research route, conditional on finding an original clue that actually supplies the event/state rule. The same SFT reproduces both location baselines. **Decision:** the single literal fixed-marker explanation is weak for these tested source registers and especially for authentic English `EA` usage; no universal language exclusion, new key, plaintext, or original-source marker operation follows. A genuinely different state/escape architecture requires an independent source selector. [Integrated evidence and limitations](../im-results/lp2-chain256-allsecret-source-gate.md#fixed-escape-symbol-word-position-gate-on-original-printed-words-agent43-12-november-2026).

**Full 256-phase GP28 predecessor-rank all-secret exclusion (12 November 2026).** The original 256-cell pp49–51 table can define a proposed repeating 256-phase key on the 28 nonzero predecessor transitions, but **no source selects that cipher architecture**. Agent13's source-conditioned `log₁₅(ΔC)`/EA marker model has within-phase equality rates **0.036688742** (whole book) and **0.037282159** (nine image chapters), each below all 104 held-out native-GP English windows (minima 0.058890995/0.058104054). A new separate complete-grid gate now grants **any 28! secret permutation in every phase**, both the natural cyclic and predecessor-sorted GP28 ranks, whole-book or source-physical-page reset, and both clock-advance/clock-hold treatments of the **86 observed immediate repeats** (F or EA reserved/erased markers). Across the eight original configurations the same-phase equal ordered-pair counts are **22,524–23,514** on **628,700–652,268** eligible ordered pairs; their maximum equality rate is below the minima from **360** source-native Shakespeare/Gibran/Emerson controls matched to the complete **2,896 original GP word lengths** and all original erasure/page positions (all controls at least **37,010** in book clocks and **38,294** in page clocks). A full **12,956-rune** GP source-native original-grid-byte-key plant decodes and re-encrypts all positions for each of sixteen fixed clock/rank/marker combinations. The original 256-byte direct source-key inverses remain nonlinguistic, with IoC below 0.04. These are **conditional exact all-secret phase-bijection exclusions**, not an unknown-page decoder; source-unselected changing states, other registers, and nonstationary keys remain open. **Original LP2 p0 exact 29! secret-state exclusion and all-page extension (12 November 2026).** A new source-exact test grants a separate arbitrary secret GP29 permutation at each observable (previous ciphertext rune, original printed word-start bit) state on physical p0 **alone**. Every one of **653,512** contiguous native 262-GP-rune excerpts across Shakespeare (218,539), Gibran (62,853), Emerson (324,369), and corrected Old English Beowulf (47,751) violates the exact functional/injective correspondence of a secret per-state alphabet, with its first contradiction occurring by original rune offset **69**. **0/653,512** passages can be the original p0 plaintext under this whole stationary cipher family, even with independent per-page keys and resets elsewhere. A separate full-original-book bound for completely arbitrary secret chapter×previous-rune tables gives at most **1,310/2,344** occurrences of any one/two selected plaintext rune identities and excludes all 89 pinned complete-book native GP English samples. Granting word-start state increases aggregate capacity but original pp40–53 locally allow only **300/537** copies of any one/two runes and exclude all 89 aligned controls. Screening all **602,736** original-length native source windows by nine-chapter E/E+O bounds retains 52 Shakespeare frequency candidates, and exact alphabet-bijection consistency rules out all 52. A fully secret **9×29×2** synthetic cipher plant reproduces and recovers all **12,956** GP29 symbols and passes the new p0-only same-state lookup. These findings are **finite-source and mechanism-specific**, leaving other English sources, moving within-page hidden state, alternate plaintext alphabets and additional cipher layers open. No unknown original LP2 plaintext or historical key recovered. **Original p23 five-dot / five-rune decoder boundary (12 November 2026).** The source-visible p23 five black dots precede a red16 heading and exactly **1,005 post-heading GP29 runes** through p26, naturally forming **201 complete quintets**. The identical five-dot graphic also appears on original p7 and solved p56 under subpixel-exact reflections/rotation, so the artwork is **not a p23-specific key or share instruction**. An independent 201-rune p2 as Shamir secret/sixth-share hypothesis also fails every original source ordering: maximum 15/201 or 21/201 required GP29 equalities, and rank-28 GF29 equations exclude **all arbitrary shared 29-symbol relabelings** under those fixed x=1..6 and layout assumptions ([source measurement and p56 transfer negative](../im-results/lp2-five-dot-three-page-art-and-phi-transfer.md)). An already existing exhaustive **732,541-projective-class GF29 Hill/linear projection test** on those printed quintets had maximum IoC **1788/40200 = 0.044477612**; the extended same all-key scan on five consecutive 201-rune strands has maximum **1770/40200 = 0.044029851**. These bound every nonconstant *stationary affine five-rune GF29 fold*, including secret output offsets, scaling and global rune relabelings, under either fixed input layout. A separately motivated historical five-of-ten Shamir interpolation search over **all 30,240 ordered original share-identifier choices x=1..10** (24,730 distinct GF29 weight vectors) peaks at **1664/40200** on each layout. None exceeds the minimum **1826/40200** among **599,769** pinned 201-rune approximate-GP English control windows, with a complete five-share original-length positive plant; the historical share decoder is only a conditional link to the 2013 Cicada protocol. The exact original 984-bit ssss v0.5 inverse also rejects p2 as secret; its natural IDs01..05 123-byte outputs miss LP2 p56 under SHA-512, SHA3-512, BLAKE2b-512 and SHAKE256(64) (0/16). More strongly, Agent21's independent C++ SFT exhausts all 120,960 source-valid 5-of-10 assignments across four layouts and tests both raw encoded and XTEA-decoded 123-byte serializations under SHA-512: 0/241,920 direct preimages. This closes the full source-valid assignment space under SHA-512, while the other three digest families were tested only for the natural assignment. The 2014 anend 56.bin witness is the same digest printed on p56. Possible key use remains untyped; no source-selected key consumer follows ([report](../im-results/lp2-p23-26-ssss201-original-format-gate.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_p23_26_ssss984_p2_original_gate_sft.cjs)). Under the finite, human-plausible Baconian operation of assigning each of the 29 rune identities one fixed unknown binary bit, exhaustive original-input search finds **164,198** legal modern 26-letter A=0..Z=25 keys and **5,224** classical 24-letter I/J, U/V-merged keys, over printed/mirrored quintets; all keys are scored in either reading direction and none reveals native-English plaintext. The **same complete 164,198-key 26-letter search** with separately trained/heldout **Latin Vulgate** and **Old English Beowulf** reference models also gives no readable original output: best source Latin scores −2729.836/−2738.608 versus native holdout −1792.066; Old English −2672.355/−2680.759 versus −1967.505. Separate authentic 201-letter Bacon positive plants win both new all-key searches. More decisively for classical Bacon, every possible fixed 29-rune bit key forces **≥40 A characters of 201** (≥45 mirrored), while **0/3,164** nonoverlapping Shakespeare/Gibran/Emerson English windows and **0/1,300** GP-normalized Latin Vulgate/Old English Beowulf windows have 40 A (**0/4,464** across five pinned controls); a planted native-English Bacon carrier decodes exactly. More strongly, **all 5,224** valid historical keys force *some letter* (even after an unknown global output-alphabet permutation) to occur **≥40/201** times in printed quintets or **≥45/201** in mirrored quintets; only **10/4,464** five-language controls reach any-letter frequency 40, and **0/4,464** reach 45. The historical mirrored decoder is therefore strongly excluded even with a secret global letter relabeling in these fixed source registers. The genuine historical **ssss 0.5** five-of-ten share algorithm uses one GF(2^(8n)) whole-secret field and a monic quintic, rather than an illustrative per-byte GF256 consumer. The 2013 participant shares were **176 bits** and are too short to carry unrestricted 201-rune LP2 strands. The earlier original capacity gate finds two source strands too large per reading only in the **optional 976-bit / 122-byte** setting. All source strands fit the program's historical **984-bit / 123-byte** field. A completed **984-bit original-cipher inverse** now tests **120,960 reconstructions** across the four natural original strand layouts/directions and every ordered selection of five IDs from the historical 01–10 set, using the separate original 201-rune p2 as either the encoded secret or the actual decoded secret (including authentic XTEA diffusion) in either direction. **Zero reconstruct original p2**; natural-order 123-byte decoded outputs are unreadable. The actual 2013 XTEA layer recovers **p7amjopgric7dfdi.onion** byte-exact, and a full original-p2-shaped 984-bit synthetic source share plant round-trips. The entire **120,960** genuine XTEA-decoded candidate-output corpus was also examined for readable secrets: most printable bytes **72/123**, longest uninterrupted printable run **16**, maximum zero bytes **6**, and **zero** complete `.onion`, `http` or `BEGIN PGP` markers. Independently, original p2 was tested as a **sixth historical share** via the exact six-point GF(2^984) monic-quintic leading-coefficient identity, across **all 1,209,600** distinct 01–10 identifier assignments, four source readings and two p2 orientations: **zero** satisfying assignments; smallest 984-bit residual Hamming weight **420**, with an exact full-p2-sized positive plant. Both exact natural SSSS roles for p2 are now excluded under these source readings and historical IDs, without excluding other unknown recipients or key material. The same source-five-dot input has no evidence for a **shared additive GP29 pad across five 201-rune strands**, including arbitrary unknown per-strand Caesar offsets: original pairwise-shift upper bounds 126/122, below 601/602 and 602/602 native controls, respectively, with an exact positive common-pad plant. An **exhaustive GP29 five-input linear/Hill inverse** enumerates all **732,541** nonzero coefficient vectors modulo nonzero scale on both printed-quintet and five-contiguous-strand layouts. Original best 201-output IoCs are **1,788/40,200 = 0.044477612** and **1,770/40,200 = 0.044029851** respectively, below all 450 pinned 201-rune GP-English strand controls (their minimum exceeds 0.05). A source-native five-by-five Hill plant recovers all five plaintext strands. This is a control-conditional direct-GP negative for any fixed invertible Hill matrix on these layouts ([exhaustive SFT](../sims/lp2_p23_26_hill_exhaustive_sft.cjs)), not a historical key. The distinct historical **ITA2** stationary rune-bit channel is exactly excluded for output limited to letters (1,121 clauses/303 exhaustive nodes), letters plus literal SPACE (944 clauses/335 nodes), or even letters+SPACE+FIGS (767 clauses/519 nodes), for **every** fixed binary assignment to the 29 rune types in either bit direction. Genuine full stateful ITA2 with NUL/LF/CR/LTRS controls, changing keys and other five-bit consumers remains possible; neither an original key nor plaintext is recovered ([exact all-secret SFT](../sims/lp2_p23_26_ita2_allmask_sft.cjs)). A further complete **SPACE=0, A=1,…,Z=26** five-bit enumeration leaves **290,417 printed** and **246,239 mirrored** secret stationary rune-bit keys; original full outputs remain nonlinguistic under a space-aware English model with successful 201-character planted controls. Even allowing an **arbitrary global permutation of all 26 output letters**, the exact original all-key test gives **zero** natural-spaced passages whose every word shares a repetition pattern with the pinned 32,232-word reference lexicon *and* whose one-letter words use at most two distinct cipher symbols (the possible A/I types). Only **446** original direction/mask outputs avoid leading/trailing/doubled spaces; just one meets the dictionary-pattern condition, but it requires five distinct single-letter symbols. These are conditional dictionary-English exclusions under a **fixed, literal SPACE=0** grammar, not a universal rejection of five-bit ciphers. A **separately specified historical ITA2 letters-mode** readout (SPACE code4, NULL/FIGS/LTRS disallowed), allowing every fixed binary assignment to 29 rune identities, has an exact all-secret limit of **12 SPACE characters per 201 quintets** in both printed and mirrored directions; a 201-character solved-LP1 English plant needs 42 and encodes exactly under the same ITA2 register. An unrestricted five-bit register allowing all teleprinter control codes instead raises the exact maximum to **36 SPACE codes** (one maximizing witness emits **54 NULLs**), so the 12-space exclusion applies only to the source-unselected uninterrupted letters-mode grammar. This excludes ordinary spaced prose only under that restricted letters-only teleprinter grammar ([complete MILP and native plant](../im-results/lp2-p23-26-ita2-space-capacity.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_p23_26_ita2_space_capacity_sft.py)). These are rigorously bounded *specific decoder-family exclusions*, not proof the five-dot illustration encodes nothing; the complete 201×5 original source input remains available for an independently selected operation. **P36–38 five-numbered-paragraph SSSS hypothesis (13 November 2026).** The original physical pages show five numbered paragraphs spanning pp36–38 (1 on p36; 2–4 on p37; 5 on p38), with **139, 91, 98, 96 and 129** GP runes respectively (**553** total). The authentic 2013 ssss v0.5 share format uses a decimal share ID and a **22-byte / 176-bit** whole-field payload (44 hex characters). Under the natural direct, lossless whole-paragraph reading, even the shortest LP2 paragraph carries **442.08 bits**, over 2.5 times one historical share; no original delimiter selects 37 GP29 digits, truncation, padding, sidecar bytes or a second decryption. Independently, five values at distinct x=1..5 always interpolate one unique degree≤4 polynomial, so a constructed 5-of-5 round-trip cannot predict a withheld share; a sixth independent share or source-known secret is required. Retire the literal direct five-paragraph / historical-share serialization, not an unknown preceding transform or a different source-selected construction. The separate numbered-instruction test #706 found no documented five-step operation: the signed 2013 TCP specification lists eight named commands, the 2014 square submission uses three squares, and obvious red-digit extractions yield only five glyphs with no held-out prediction. **#705 is closed:** eight pinned pre-2014 source editions (147,914 tokens), including the Babylonian-artwork-motivated 1903 Johns translation of *The Code of Hammurabi*, yielded no 12-word/40-rune candidate with five held-out following words; the longest exact word-length match remained eight words. The cuneiform artwork supplies no author-selected title, translator or law number. No crib, original plaintext or cipher transport was recovered ([report](../im-results/lp2-pp36-38-numbered-aphorism-crib-gate.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_pp36_38_numbered_aphorism_crib_sft.cjs)). [Bounded original-source decision, #688](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/688).

**Original p2/p23–26 five-stream all-secret GP29 algebraic obstructions (12 November 2026).** The actual p2 contains exactly **201 GP29 runes**, using all 29 symbols; after p23's five-dot-marked red16 heading, p23–26 contain **1,005** runes, permitting **five 201-symbol strands or 201 five-rune quintets**. This alignment is source-visible but not a confirmed cipher link. Two complementary complete original-input impossibility theorems now cover both fixed layouts and independent source/target reading reversals. **(1) Five arbitrary secret per-lane lookup tables summed mod29, followed by any unknown output permutation:** 172 original equal-rune constraints in 145 unknown lookup values give rank **139** on quintets and **140** on strands. The only null freedoms are five constant functions and one unused lane symbol in the quintet layout. Thus all compatible outputs are constant, contradicting the 29 original target identities. **(2) Any stationary five-input GP29 polynomial of total degree at most four, including arbitrary cross-lane coefficients, followed by any unknown output permutation:** the corresponding 172×126 quartic constraint matrix has rank **125** in every orientation; its only solution is the constant polynomial. Degrees one, two and three likewise reach ranks **5/20/55**, their exact constant-only ceilings. In contrast, the degree-five source evaluation matrix has row rank **201**, admitting an exact but uninformative polynomial interpolant for any 201-symbol target. Genuine nonlinear source-native positive plants for both theorem families distinguish success from constant-fitting. The key-independent exclusions apply only to this original ciphertext-to-ciphertext pairing under the stated stationary additive or low-degree polynomial families. Arbitrary evolving state, nonlinear secret input relabelings with cross-stream interaction and alternative framing are unresolved. [Consolidated original-cipher proof](../im-results/lp2-p2-p23-five-stream-separable-rank-obstruction.md) · [All-secret separable SFT](../sims/lp2_p2_p23_five_stream_separable_rank_sft.cjs) · [Sharp polynomial degree SFT](../sims/lp2_p2_p23_polynomial_degree_gate_sft.cjs). No unknown plaintext, authentic key or destination is recovered.

**Original-source sixteen-page floral-frame common-key obstruction (12 November 2026).** On the 15 rune-bearing physical pages p40–55, the copied frame does not support a shared page-position GP29 additive pad over ordinary native-GP prose, **even with arbitrarily hidden per-page Caesar offsets**. Across 105 aligned original page pairs there are **684/19,079** exact matches, and the pairwise best of all 29 secret offsets yields only **1,232/19,079 = 0.06457361**, below **every one of 6,000** matched-length GP English controls (2,000 each Shakespeare, Gibran, Emerson; minimum best-shift control **0.06834740**). All-secret pad cancellation makes the statistic key-independent; a planted genuine shared-pad GP text preserves it exactly. This is a bounded native-text necessary-invariant negative, **not an exclusion of pagewise distinct keys, feedback, non-GP registers or further encryption layers**. [Original source-constrained test](../im-results/lp2-floral-section-sharedpad-invariant.md) · [single-file SFT](../sims/lp2_floral_sharedpad_invariant_sft.cjs).

**P40 red-heading eight-column all-secret exclusion (12 November 2026).** The original p40 red heading supplies **eight distinct GP29 rune values** and a unique sorted 8-column key order, followed within its source-visible pp40–53 chapter by **3,000 = 8×375 runes**, while the numerical table has **eight printed columns**. For classical columnar transposition, whether the terminal p53 red51 is retained (3,000 runes) or excluded (2,949 ragged-column runes), **every** original ciphertext column under both consecutive-block and stride-eight interpretations has entropy at least **4.769002166 bits/rune**. Across **4,683** GP-native Shakespeare/Gibran/Emerson source windows sampled every127 runes at each length, the largest natural-language column entropy is **4.391099679**. This gives a key-independent, reference-conditional exclusion of **every independent secret *static* 29-way column substitution** at the frozen eight-column clock: relabeling cannot change any column entropy. The eight-rune heading/column alignment is a motivated clue, but no plaintext or authentic key follows; a time-varying per-table-cell rule requires a separate invariant ([source report](../im-results/lp2_p40_red8_allsecret_columnar_entropy.md); [positive-controlled SFT](../sims/lp2_p40_red8_allsecret_columnar_entropy_sft.cjs)). The superficially matching 14-page/14-column historical hypothesis remains unselected: the 14 physical pages include numeric-only p50, and the 3,000-rune body leaves a four-symbol tail under width14. Original scans support reading the p42, p45, p49 and p52 leading one-rune fragments as **cross-page word carries**, not standalone singleton words. The canonical continuous parser already includes these as four of the manuscript's **32 cross-page words**: p41→p42 = ᛟᛟᚳᛏᛄᛞ, p44→p45 = ᚱᛗᛞᛋᚳ, p48→p49 = ᛇᚠᚢᛞᚣᚪᛚᚠᛡ, and p51→p52 = ᛈᛚᚠᛈ. Their body offsets after the p40 red eight-rune heading are **496, 1315, 2400 and 2558**. This matches the p54→p55 carry; p53→p54 is a distinct chapter boundary closed by punctuation and the red13 mark. Crucially, a carry remains a ciphertext rune: all four symbols stay in the 3,000-rune stream, so its length is still **3,000 = 14×214 + 4**, not 2,996. Deleting the four runes to force a rectangle is invalid. No original LP2 clue defines a four-symbol tail rule or an identity-changing stage after the historical 2014 permutation, so the 14-column transform is not run and the transfer remains unselected ([page-boundary report](../im-results/lp2-p40-53-page-boundary-carry-gate.md); [reproducer](../sims/lp2_p40_53_page_carry_gate.cjs); [canonical 32-word parser](../sims/lp2_word_pages.cjs); [#719](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/719)).

**P40–53 original nonzero-step and composite inverse gates (13 November 2026).** The exact 3,000-rune p40–p53 body retains **18/18** adjacent-equality zero steps. A frozen terminal predecessor-exclusion transform (IV = last red-heading rune), sorted-red8 eight-column inverse and row-major table-byte subtraction modulo28 re-encrypts all 3,000 symbols and passes native plants, but the original output remains ciphertext-like (GP29 IoC **0.035371346** versus raw **0.034544404** and native Gibran **0.059365344**); retire that exact composite, not all stateful or column-local architectures ([report](../im-results/lp2-p40-gp28-chain-grid-columnar-original-gate.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_p40_gp28_chain_grid_columnar_sft.cjs); [#696](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/696)). Separately, the original nonzero GP29 difference channel's first 16 values uniquely force a stationary degree-eight GF29 recurrence that already predicts the 17th nonzero step incorrectly (22 versus 27); it matches only **61/1,491** held-out original steps, while the planted control predicts exactly. Retire this fixed degree-eight generator, not nonlinear or source-selected state models ([report](../im-results/lp2-p40-delta8-original-nonzero-step-recurrence-gate.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_p40_delta8_nonzero_lfsr_sft.cjs); [#693](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/693)). A distinct pre-columnar per-column state model remains unselected and is gated by #700; the repeat-count change alone does not select layer order.

**Exact word-length source matching with solved-page calibration (12 November 2026).** All **2,896** complete printed words in the 12,956-rune unknown LP2 corpus can be indexed by their original GP29 rune lengths, preserving the source word/line/page clock. The expanded screen covers **14 pinned profiles**, including a second GP29 English normalization of the historically selected 2012 Cicada book-code source, the *Mabinogion* (PG5160 story slice): its specific *Lady of the Fountain* chapter has a longest exact run of **nine words**; the full selected story section's 11-word maximum is ordinary under 250 shuffled-profile controls (median10, p95=12, 78/250≥11). This null-calibrates the same 11/9 maxima found under the earlier A–Z/longest-match Mabinogion profile. The 2012 second-chance *Marriage of Heaven and Hell* image and 2012 Gibson *Agrippa* remain distinct book-code sources; the 2013 Crowley *Liber AL vel Legis* chapters remain at a maximum exact run of **eight words**. Shakespeare, Gibran, Emerson, Blake, Latin Vulgate, Old English Beowulf and solved LP1/LP2 text remain in the fixed comparison set. The independently solved p57's full **20-word** printed length sequence is reproduced exactly by the English PARABLE reference and recovered when planted. This narrows only exact quotation from these texts under intact word boundaries; it does not select a book key or recover unknown plaintext ([consolidated report](../im-results/lp2-wordlength-source-matching-gate.md); [Mabinogion profile](../resources/external/cicada2012-mabinogion-gutenberg5160-gp-wordlengths.json); [SFT](../sims/lp2_mabinogion_wordlength_gate.cjs); [earlier 2012-bookcode SFT](../sims/lp2_2012_bookcode_wordlength_sft.cjs)).

**Expanded public-domain quotation gate (13 November 2026).** A new byte-pinned corpus of **18 GITenberg editions / 1,094,603 Latin-letter words** was searched against the original p40, p42 and p53 printed word-length shapes under the frozen GP multigraph normalization. The best original match is **nine consecutive words**; there are **zero** original matches of at least **12 consecutive words and 40 GP runes**. All 18 source-native planted 34-word controls recover exactly, so the method detects the required long matches. This retires verbatim long quotations from these 18 editions only, not all literature, paraphrase, nonliteral borrowing or other source corpora ([report](../im-results/lp2-quote18-publicdomain-corpus-gate.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_quote18_publicdomain_sft.cjs); [#691](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/691)).

**P40 eight-share GF29 five-of-eight consistency (12 November 2026).** The original pp40–53 chapter begins with eight red GP29 runes followed by exactly **3,000 = 8×375** runes. As a clue-constrained native-GP adaptation of Cicada's earlier (different-field) five-share mechanism, we tested degree≤4 GF29 sharing using red-rune ordinal+1 or historical sequential share IDs across original strided, contiguous, and red-key-ranked eight-stream layouts. **None of 2,250 original eight-share rows is a valid degree-four codeword** (zero complete valid rows in all six cases); the fifth-share-derived predictions match only **25–49 of 1,125** withheld original symbols per layout. All six independently planted 375-rune solved-GP positive controls recover **375/375** runes and predict **1,125/1,125** shares; unrestricted eight-point interpolation trivially roundtrips all 3,000 original symbols. Granting **one corrupt share per eight-symbol group** still leaves only **1–6/375** original groups compatible in each variant, whereas the damaged native GP plant passes in all **375/375** groups. This retires these exact six direct GF29 five-of-eight consumers, without an authenticated threshold/field instruction, new plaintext, key or final destination ([full proof](../im-results/lp2-p40-red8-shamir5-invariant.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_p40_red8_shamir5_sft.cjs)).

**P40 red-eight attached-boundary transport exclusion (13 November 2026).** The original p40–53 body holds **3,000** GP runes with **667** printed words and only **13 one-rune words**. If each printed word-final flag travels attached to its preceding rune through a classical eight-column transposition, the source red-heading inverse produces **668 words with 153 one-rune words**; an exhaustive **40,320-order enumeration** proves **every** eight-column key yields at least **122** one-rune words (median153, maximum180). By comparison all **147,071** pinned GP-native Shakespeare/Gibran/Emerson complete 668-word windows contain at most **72** one-rune words (maximum by author 72/43/50). A native whole-word 3,000-bit plant round-trips exactly. This is a **finite-language, key-independent exclusion of rune-attached punctuation transport**, leaving standard space-stripping/re-spacing, word-local transpositions, and alternate languages open. The prior red-heading 101 repeated-adjacent-rune statistic is also ordinary among all 8! orders: 11,424 of 40,320 yield ≤101. Neither observation identifies an authentic cipher or recovers LP2 plaintext. [Source analysis](../im-results/lp2-p40-columnar-antirepeat-layer-order.md) · [Original-input CJS SFT](../sims/lp2_p40_columnar_antirepeat_order_sft.cjs).

**P40 layer-order and word/punctuation selector decisions (13 November 2026).** The source-gated independent-column predecessor model has no selected eight initial states, transition or repeat-escape rule (#700); the historical 2014 Stage05 operation does not supply them. A separate word-local/punctuation-aware inverse gate finds no original instruction selecting how spaces or word-final flags are stripped, moved or restored (#711). The already reported exhaustive 40,320-order singleton and repeat statistics remain the finite exclusion; they do not identify a new cipher. Only a new authenticated boundary/escape instruction should reopen this chapter.

**P40 32×8 all-secret repeating table-cell alphabets (12 November 2026; Director #676).** The same original red-eight columnar layout yields **3,000 = 375×8** runes against the **32×8** printed table. Grant an *arbitrary independent nonlinear GP29 bijection at each of all 256 table cells*, reused every 32 rows. Equal ciphertext runes at the same phase/cell would require equal plaintext runes for **every one of (29!)^256 secret keys**. Original decolumned ciphertext has only **581/16,104 = 0.0360779930** same-phase pairs (the direct stride-eight alternative: **564/16,104**), versus **at least 0.0510432** in all **4,683** matched contiguous GP-native Shakespeare/Gibran/Emerson controls. A full reversible 256-secret-permutation plant reproduces its native phase collisions and recovers 3,000/3,000 symbols. Thus even a fresh non-additive 32-row-cycle S-box per grid cell cannot directly recover ordinary GP29 prose; an independently evidenced changing-state operation or different intermediate register would be required. This **strictly extends** the earlier eight-static-alphabet obstruction but does not eliminate arbitrary feedback or a non-native language ([exact theorem and decision](../im-results/lp2-p40-grid256-allsecret-phase-equality.md); [source-native SFT](../sims/lp2_p40_grid256_allsecret_phase_equality_sft.cjs)).

**Original-boundary joint-output impossibility (12 November 2026; Director #603).** On 12,901 original within-page rune transitions, classify the *actual printed* boundary-before-rune as a binary output alongside the next ciphertext rune. After fixing the previous ciphertext rune q, original pp0–39 contain up to **58** different (boundary bit, next rune) output pairs, and the untouched pp40–55 holdout up to **48**; **all 29 predecessor-q states** exceed 29 distinct pairs in each split. The original pp40–53 chapter alone reaches 47. A deterministic transducer with only the current **one GP29 plaintext rune** as variable input and q as state can emit at most **29** different pairs, independently of every secret codebook or key. Therefore an encoder cannot generate **all** printed word boundaries *from just the current plaintext rune and q*. If plaintext word segmentation is independently supplied as input, or a hidden/page/position state exists, the theorem does not apply. The narrower complete one-special-symbol boundary-escape GP29→28 model additionally fails at **63 repeated ciphertext transitions without a printed word boundary** and multiple boundary followers for every q. The native GP29 reversible plant checks full 12,956-rune input. This is a source-only model exclusion, **no plaintext or authentic key**, and does not disprove general word-boundary information capacity ([source report](../im-results/lp2_boundary_single_escape_symbol_source_gate.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_boundary_single_escape_symbol_source_gate_sft.cjs)).

**Visible word-boundary-state strengthening (12 November 2026).** In original pp40–53, the source state (visual chapter, immediately previous ciphertext rune GP18/E, inside a printed word) occurs 91 times and has **all 29** possible next runes. Thus adding an arbitrarily keyed word-start bit to the earlier chapter+previous-ciphertext state still requires a **29-symbol** source alphabet; first/last flags require at least28, word-position parity at least28, and word-length parity at least27 under their corresponding frozen state models. This excludes raw-Latin26 directly in those entire all-secret deterministic one-symbol families, without assuming English or a key. More detailed word-position/page state survives; no plaintext is recovered ([exact original gate](../im-results/lp2-word-boundary-feedback-register-capacity.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_word_boundary_feedback_capacity_sft.cjs)).

**Original-language-independent alphabet-capacity bound (12 November 2026).** Under **any** deterministic one-GP-rune-in/one-rune-out encoder with state limited to (original visible chapter, immediately previous ciphertext rune), the source alphabet must contain **all 29 distinct symbols**. In the original p40–53 chapter, **six predecessor states** are each followed by **all 29 distinct GP runes**, and **217/261** observed chapter×predecessor states have more than 16 possible successors. This excludes directly mapping a 16-rune Younger Futhark, 16-digit hexadecimal or other smaller alphabet through that entire all-secret feedback family, independently of English language statistics or key values. Original first-order states cannot generate 29 distinct outputs from 16 inputs. With a second preceding ciphertext rune, all **12,956** original unknown-page runes remain formally compatible with a size-16 source alphabet and arbitrary injective state tables: explicit reset sentinels yield **5,699 page-reset/chapter-shared states** or **5,675 chapter-continuous states**, both with maximum next-output support 11. A word-reset model keyed only by visible chapter and prior two ciphertext runes is incompatible with a sixteen-symbol source: eight chapters exhibit all **29** next outputs at the beginning-of-word reset state (word marks taken from the rune-identical derivative transcription; no original cipher reset is established). A stronger **fixed shared 16-codebook GP29 additive-shift** family fails on just four original p40–53 history states: every one of **29³ = 24,389** relative shifts requires a combined support of at least **17**. The independently motivated p0 **Younger Futhark 16-symbol inventory**, when rendered in GP29 using conventional letters and K→C, fails even the more permissive *arbitrary state-specific affine* family: six original state supports are impossible under all 812 invertible affine maps; at `7:0,24`, the observed eleven output symbols intersect any affine image of that fixed alphabet in at most ten. Even allowing **any single GP rune replacement** in the sixteen-symbol source set, all **208** alternative codebooks fail against the same six original state supports under every affine transform. This excludes that precise **conditional cross-register representation**, while the original p0 mark itself still selects only a local TH candidate. By contrast, a *ciphertext-fitted* shared 16-codebook with freely independent **affine** multiplication-and-offset mappings at 5,699 states exactly decodes and re-encodes all 12,956 original positions, with no meaningful source plaintext or authenticated state key. In that fixed-codebook affine construction, **2,193** observed singleton history states each permit all 16 source labels independently, so the *same original ciphertext* has at least **2^8,772 distinct full-length 16-symbol plaintexts** under the freely keyed family. This exact lower bound makes a fitted inverse without independent key selection cryptanalytically uninformative. A source-selected p50-table cross-register gate further rejects the direct 16x16 uses motivated by p0's 16-symbol inventory: direct bigram labels have no path, all 256 seeds fail the tested second-order additive and affine schedules, and the invertible GF29 Hill inverse remains at IoC≈0.0345. The tested byte parameterizations were high/low nibbles and base29 quotient/remainder, with both table orientations and signs. These are exact architectural compatibility/exclusion results; the affine fit explicitly demonstrates underdetermination. [Reconciled source-register decision](../im-results/lp2-source16-twohistory-codebook-decision.md) · [Original full-position, four-state and Younger Futhark SFT](../sims/lp2_second_history_16_register_sft.cjs). No original source yet selects any complete alternate plaintext register or actual decoder. [Original-source alphabet-capacity theorem and positive plant](../im-results/lp2-alternative-register-source-gate.md#12-november-2026-source-visible-chapter-alphabet-capacity-test-480) · [Reproducing CJS SFT](../sims/lp2_visible_chapter_register_capacity_sft.cjs).

**GP29 first-order top-twelve majorization extension (13 November 2026).** The earlier [12 November all-secret GP29 predecessor-state gate](../im-results/lp2_chapter_predecessor_allsecret_capacity.md) had already evaluated fully arbitrary 29! secret state tables, 1- and 2-letter capacities, and exact English-source p0/full-book contradictions. This additional work computes the previously unreported **top-twelve GP29 majorization bound**, a separate inner-red-heading map and an independent historical Blake source control. Each fixed prior-ciphertext state preserves its outgoing frequency multiset, so the sum of that state's largest k original ciphertext counts bounds the total possible occurrences of any k plaintext rune types. The complete 12,956-rune original stream yields a single-symbol upper bound of **717** and top-twelve bound **6,769** under one shared first-order secret table; fixed page resets give **719 / 6,784**, whole-word resets **695 / 6,671**. With **nine independent secret visual-chapter tables**, and even with **all 55 physical page-opening runes individually unconstrained**, the previously known top-one bound is **1,310 (10.11115%)** and the new top-twelve bound is **9,006 (69.51220%)**. The fixed-reset-sentinel version of this family has the stricter top-one bound **1,269 (9.79469%)**. A competing original red-heading nine-text segmentation has stricter maxima **1,256 / 8,911**. The source-native solved LP1 reference has **406/3,287 (12.35169%) E** and **2,552/3,287 (77.63918%) top twelve**; a second historically linked 1906 Blake English source yields **5,623/5,623 complete 12,956-rune windows** whose E count is at least **1,607** and top-twelve count at least **9,974**, both beyond these bounds; these exact frequencies are incompatible with every tested first-order architecture regardless of its hidden keys. The unconditional theorem is the numeric capacity bound, rather than an all-English exclusion: unknown compressed, flatter-frequency, non-GP, variable-length or more-than-one-history plaintexts lie outside it. Indeed, allowing two preceding ciphertext runes gives a generous **2,021 / 12,034** capacity under one shared table, so this family remains possible without an identified original key. This is a new source-constraining *negative*, with no recovered authentic plaintext or decoder. [Consolidated proof, complete nine-variant table, and two-history control](../im-results/lp2-firstorder-gp29-majorization-allkey.md) · [Original-source CJS SFT](../sims/lp2_firstorder_gp29_capacity_sft.cjs) · [passing expanded native CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43380890708).
