**p32 prime-rank boundary (exact correspondence; not a route).** The archived 2016 \`4gq25.jpg\` dimensions (563×569) match first-row/first-column GP-prime sums only after the 121 p32 runes are reflowed into an **unprinted 11×11 row-major matrix**; their prime ranks 103/104 select one-based p32 rune positions 103–104, a complete ciphertext word \`EN\` followed by \`NW…\`. Zero-based offsets 103–104 select \`NN\` across the word boundary. The GP-prime sum of \`EN\` is 96 and φ(96)=32, a self-address to p32. Neither the dimension→prime-rank→rune coordinate rule nor a compass start, orientation, step count, or stop condition is source-selected; these are ciphertext transliterations, not recovered directions. An additional cross-round comparison of the original 2012 dimensions (503,509) and 2016 dimensions (563,569), sorted by size, gives prime ranks [96,97]→[103,104], a +7 shift on **both** axes. Since p32's image checksums fix both axes, this nominates both row 7 and column 7, not a unique column. The column's 89/463 match to p15's F89→prime(90)=463 appears only after inspecting p32 contents; the equally selected row 7 sums to 577=prime(106), outside p15's Fibonacci-selected ordinal set. The 2012 source uses raw dimensions in a product, while p15's prime-index rule is Fibonacci-specific; neither source instructs prime-counting image dimensions or differencing their ranks. The alternative 103−13=90 is likewise unselected because the 13-dot mark recurs. Retain the exact arithmetic, but retire the +7/column-7 selector; the direct period-11 Vigenère test is already negative ([cross-round source-gate report](../im-results/2016-image-dimensions-lag6-source-derivation.md); [SFT](../sims/p32_column7_p15_fibonacci89_source_gate.cjs)).

[Three source clocks and image evidence](../workplace/lp2-section-clock-ambiguity.md). A retrospective full-period 13/31 LCG candidate over the 32 thirteen-dot-delimited full-text fragments can cyclically order the solved p56/p57 tail, but its previously highlighted exact dual order depended on the **unsourced seed x₀=0**. Across all 32 possible seeds of the same 13/31 cycle, the forward solved-tail restriction takes all four cyclic rotations; the independently source-derived FOLD value **18** used as a seed gives ordinary chronological forward tail **[28,29,30,31]** and inverse-position tail **[30,29,28,31]**, rather than the seed-zero **[31,28,29,30]** dual match. No original instruction selects an LCG, zero seed, or downstream state interpretation. Direct transposition, 40 literal grid-byte inverses, and a 576-model 13/31 LCG row/column DES-key integration do not improve held-out unknown-text language score; the DES positive control recovers its 12,956-rune native plant at rank one. An independent **all-secret public-phase obstruction** uses the original 32 fragments×8 table columns: its within-phase equality rate is **94,652/2,728,348 = 0.0346921**, far below **0.05675–0.07807** across 2,335 native-GP windows from modern English, Latin and Old English, invariant under all eight source row/column/clock relabellings and any secret 29-way per-phase bijection (planted controls pass). This excludes fixed-public-phase direct native-GP29 substitution, while hidden feedback, non-GP registers, and other transformations remain open; treat the LCG as an unresolved indexing/clock clue, not a decryption ([reproducer and report](../im-results/lp2_lcg32_fulltext_fragment_order.md)).

## 1. Reproducible source facts

### Historical 2014 handoff and the separate AN-END target

The 2014 recruitment chain comprised seven onions: `auqgnxjtvdbll3pv` → `cu343l33nqaekrnw` → `fv7lyucmeozzd5j4` → `avowyfgl5lkzfj3n` → `q4utgdi2n4m4uim5` → `ut3qtzbrvs7dtvzp` → `ky2khlqdf7qdznac`. The Onion 5 GEB book code yielded the Sixth Onion, `ut3qtzbrvs7dtvzp.onion`; it served four initial Liber Primus pages and later requested three magic squares (orders/sums 5/3301, 5/1033, 7/1033) plus a Tor hidden-service URL. The seventh onion, `ky2khlqdf7qdznac.onion`, published Liber Primus. The final returned rune image was decoded with Vigenère key `CIRCUMFERENCE`. This is solved historical context, not a key for LP2 0–55 and not the AN-END target. See [`results/2014-onion-chain.md`](2014-onion-chain.md).

The 05.jpg trailer's 179-byte OOB data is the already documented Onion 3 payload that reverses to the 5×5 magic square of sum 1033; it is not a new p55 key. See [`im-results/lp1-p05-trailer-mobius-matrix.md`](../im-results/lp1-p05-trailer-mobius-matrix.md) for the corrected reproduction.

Keep that historical onion distinct from the solved AN-END page's separate 512-bit target:
`36367763ab73783c7af284446c59466b4cd653239a311cb7116d4618dee09a8425893dc7500b464fdaf1672d7bef5e891c6e2274568926a49fb4f45132c2a8b4`.
The AN-END hash algorithm and preimage remain unknown; the direct Ed25519 onion-key interpretation failed, and the external [AN-END hunt findings](https://github.com/Dukotah/cicada3301/blob/master/liber-primus/analysis/anend_hunt/FINDINGS.md) reconstruct the seven-onion chain and report that the AN-END address is gated behind solving the preceding LP2 pages. Do not label the Sixth Onion as the AN-END page.


### Language and register scope

A source-register correction changes an earlier Old English control: the 10 November `beowulf_oe_gp_profile.json` representation silently dropped most lowercase thorn/eth/ash characters, yielding an artificially tiny TH register (**94** out of 45,299); full Unicode-aware normalization gives **2,078 TH and 560 AE in 48,012** GP runes. The corrected 20-period Old English GP29 inversion stays negative on independent LP2 pages (best held-out **−0.80053 nats/rune**), with source-native plants recovered. The legacy fixture is retained solely for historical reproduction, while [the corrected source and experiment](../im-results/beowulf_old_english_periodic_inverse.md) is authoritative for this finite model. Separate Old English-dependent summary statistics require checking which source version they used before comparing their numerical thresholds.

The directly demonstrated plaintext/clue registers in the known Cicada and solved-manuscript material are English: Blake verse selected by the 2012 MIDI clue, LP1 A-KOAN dialogue, short Cicada signed prose, and the solved LP2 p56/p57 anchors. This does not prove that all 56 unknown LP2 pages are English. The [source-grounded GP29 benchmark](../im-results/source-register-benchmark.md) shows that the existing broad English controls are adequate for these tested alternatives at matched training size; the LP1 A-KOAN model narrowly wins its own held-out text, while broad English models remain close. No source-supported non-English candidate language emerged. A source-led review of the original p33 cuneiform numeral header and p0 3|3 cipher-rune marks finds no complete alternative register: the former selects only 1033/3301 sexagesimal numbers, while the latter locally selects Younger Futhark þ but supplies no GP29-to-16-rune mapping ([bounded source gate](../im-results/lp2-alternative-register-source-gate.md)). #480 CLOSED: no source-selected alternate language/register or complete segmentation survives. The p37 red Arabic 2/3/4 margin numerals are editorial paragraph labels. A language-independent bound also finds 217/261 (chapter, preceding-cipher) states with >16 distinct next runes; in pp40–53, six predecessor symbols each precede all 29 GP runes, so a deterministic one-source-symbol→one-cipher-rune encoder cannot use a 16-symbol source alphabet with only visible chapter and previous-rune state. Expanded hidden state/block or variable-length encodings remain open.

**The solved p57 PARABLE has an authenticated primary-source text carrier.** The complete plaintext is reproduced verbatim in the original 2013 761.MP3 ID3v2.3 TXXX user-text frame (native frame offset 10; 206-byte frame; literal backslash-n clause separators). Its TXXX numeric field 1,595,277,641 is exactly 1,031×1,229×1,259, the product of the three p57 body-clause GP-prime sums; the title “The Instar Emergence” has GP-prime sum 761, matching the filename. This is Cicada's own 2013 source text, not an unattributed Gibran/Blake/Liber AL passage. Six source-natural number/totient/clause encodings under both direct additive signs on p55's 43 strict interiors are nonlinguistic; no p55 key follows. The ID3 frame is fully audited; the 6,397-frame LAME3.98r sample stream has no appended data or source-selected waveform instruction (#442/#445). The terminal 23-dot mark does not select a 23-of-31 Shamir reconstruction: the authenticated 2013 SSSS precedent is 5-of-10, and LP2 supplies no 31 share labels, x-coordinate convention, field or polynomial; the 4/648 exact-prediction observation is not a key (#501 CLOSED; [gate](../im-results/p57-23of31-share-selector-gate.md)). 


**2013 second-onion ICMP stage (historical, not LP2).** The replacement host `li498-122.members.linode.com` is mapped to `106.187.103.122` in the preserved 2013 IRC transcript (participant record, not recovered from a PCAP). The signed “knock on the sky and listen” clue led participants to inspect ICMP: the responder is described as returning one data line per ping, with concurrent pingers splitting the stream and some networks dropping the second reply. The 28-line, 778-byte hex stream decompresses byte-for-byte to the 959-byte PGP-signed message naming only `pklmx2eeh6fjt7zf.onion`. Three tcpdump samples align exactly to data lines 19–21; their ICMP identifiers 3457, 3461, and 3463 fit a strong 3301-seeded next-prime counter, predicting 28 identifiers from 3307 through 3527. Only those three positions are observed; the original PCAP remains missing. The gzip mtime and PGP signature time both equal 10 January 2013 17:07:15 UTC; the “knock” hint is signed 14 January 05:31:07 UTC and the IRC log records public discovery on 15 January. The tcpdump sample's 17:32 timestamps have no date. The pklmx continuation is a separate 2013 coordinate/poster/SSSS branch, not LP2 plaintext or a key; see [the ICMP reconstruction](../im-results/2013-icmp-echo-packet-reconstruction.md) and [the pklmx gate](../im-results/pklmx-2013-onion-continuation-gate.md). The separately reconstructed 2013 pklmx Shamir poster chain has **seven source-recorded shares consistent with one degree-five polynomial**: signed share identifier 10 is decimal **x=10**, as in the original ssss input parser, rather than hexadecimal x=16. All 21 five-share subsets recover the same secret; the previous 'outlier share' interpretation was a reproducer bug, not a Cicada decoy. This historical correction does not supply an LP2 key ([pklmx source audit](../im-results/pklmx-2013-onion-continuation-gate.md); [Issue #396](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/396)).

The page-15 spiral is prior Cicada-community research. The original file structures and annotations are useful fixed inputs for testing hypotheses. They have not unlocked LP2.

**Retired p50 four-ring ↔ terminal-page source selector (Issue #391; ring arithmetic retained).** The printed 32×8 table has exact nested ring lengths **76/68/60/52**; p55 has 76 runes and p53 has 128 black plus 51 red. However the proposed p53 68/60 cut falls **inside** a printed three-rune word, and three distinct source-visible one-event additions can complete 256 positions (p53 terminal 13-dot, p54 enlarged Ac24, p55 terminal mark). No source selects the extra event, traversal orientation or receiver; the competing p55+p56+p57=256 frame is equally available. Exact 16-layout original-input tests already reject direct fixed byte→GP maps (≥194 residue-map changes, ≥87 raw-byte-map changes). **Retire the selected ring-to-page decoding interpretation absent independent source instructions**, rather than treating the numerical length correspondence as an LP2 key ([original-image source-selector audit](../im-results/terminal-grid-xor-anend-digest.md#alternative-four-nested-grid-rings-aligned-with-pp5355)).

**GP29 Futhorc phonetic-map gate (no distinct alternate cipher alphabet).** Millward & Hayes Table 5.3 gives the 29-sign English Futhorc equivalent values and probable phonetic values. Its `y [j]` and `h [x]` are the same phonemes already represented by GP `J` and `X`; `œ/æ` are the diacritic forms of GP `OE/AE`. The only potentially substantive difference is Ior `io` versus `ia`, which the source explicitly leaves unresolved; Eoh is also marked uncertain. Applying the table's equivalent labels to the original 12,956 unknown runes changes only 1,378 display labels (J→Y 450, X→H 458, IA→IO 470); p57 has zero of these symbols and cannot select the alternatives. The rune-index stream is unchanged, so this is a transcription convention, not a decoder. [Source audit](../im-results/lp2-futhorc-phonetic-map-gate.md) · [reproducer](../sims/lp2_futhorc_phonetic_map_gate.cjs).

**Old English Rune Poem / p14 illustration codebook gate (13 November 2026).** The 29-stanza Old English Rune Poem follows the same 29-rune Futhorc order as GP29, so the source fixes identity indexing only; no stanza-content operation, key schedule or alternate substitution is selected. The p14 mayfly/day-fly ↔ Dæg and sedge ↔ Eolhx association is a plausible semantic cross-reference, but the sedge raster is reused on pp10/12/14 and supplies no page-specific selector. The frozen p14 operation test and full 29-stanza codebook test recover no unknown plaintext; retain the literary association as a hypothesis, not a decoder ([#697](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/697); [#704](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/704)).

## 2. Strongest manuscript-wide ciphertext constraints

**Boundary dependence.** The 55 rune-bearing unknown pages contain **12,956 runes** and **86/12,901** repeated pairs when only within-page successor positions are counted. In the nine-section stream (retaining page joins but excluding eight section joins), the same 86 events span **12,947 transitions**: **63/10,060** inside complete words and **23/2,887** between adjacent words within sections. The full concatenation has **12,955 transitions**, including **54 page joins** and **8 master-segment joins**, with zero equal pairs at either boundary; line joins are **4/593**. Original-image 13-dot annotations bracket **25 unique rune transitions** (zero equal pairs), including all eight master-segment joins. A [source-delimiter classification](../im-results/lp2-doublet-boundary-audit.md) finds only **60** pairs that are consecutive characters in the transcription with no intervening delimiter; the remaining **26** cross a printed line break within a word (**3**), a word delimiter on the same line (**22**), or both (**1**). This distinction matters for visual allograph tests, where flattened-stream adjacency need not mean neighboring physical glyphs. The observed **23** cross-word equalities are far below the **99.806** expectation when original words are shuffled within sections. Under the complementary exact page-multiset permutation null, those **23** occur against expected **97.746** (overlap-aware z **−7.698**), while the 63 within-word repeats occur against **341.420**; the normalized rate contrast is only **z=0.4555**, with no resolved change in suppression strength. The deficit survives more constrained rearrangements. A mechanism must account for both within-word and across-word behavior, including [physical line breaks](../im-results/physical-linebreak-continuity.md). [Word-boundary experiment](../im-results/cross-word-anti-repeat-constraint.md).

**All-secret periodic direct-GP substitution equality gate (12 November 2026).** Every direct, length-preserving, period-T GP29 cipher with an arbitrary secret **29! symbol bijection at each repeated key phase** must preserve equality of plaintext symbols exactly T positions apart within its reset segment, regardless of the secret tables. On the exact 12,956 original unsolved runes, all **60,000** comparisons for book periods 1–6,000, all **10,000** comparisons for nine-section-reset periods 1–1,000, and all **2,500** comparisons for physical-page-reset periods 1–250 fall **below** the same-lag equality of ten fixed, 12,956-rune native-GP Shakespeare/Gibran control windows. Across all 72,500 matched comparisons, the smallest control-minus-original gap is **+0.00116768** at lag2; all 3 planted unknown-29!-table encryptions recover **12,956/12,956** plaintext runes and the invariant exactly. This finite original-source-language gate disfavors short stationary-phase direct GP29 ciphers even with wholly unrestricted secret alphabets; it does not exclude altered plaintext registers, state-dependent phase drift, skips or long/nonperiodic keys, and no original plaintext is recovered. [Exact original gate, ten source windows and all-secret controls](../im-results/lp2-period-equality-allsecret-original.md) · [Reproducer](../sims/lp2_period_equality_allsecret_original_sft.cjs).

**All-secret pagewise ciphertext-feedback capacity (new bounded family exclusion).** For every one-, two- or three-tap GF29 linear feedback recurrence on the *previous ciphertext*, grant **each of 55 nonempty original pages** its own completely arbitrary 29-rune secret substitution (all 29! bijections), any of the 29^m tap vectors, and m unconstrained initial symbols. Exhausting all coefficients in the original 12,956 runes yields a sharp **E+O combined-frequency upper bound of 1,842 / 2,104 / 2,348 runes** for tap order m=1/2/3. In the independently pinned Gibran/Shakespeare/Emerson GP29 controls, E and O are the two most frequent runes in all three, and **every** exact-length contiguous window contains at least **2,556 / 2,430 / 2,455** E+O runes. Thus these page-stationary families cannot directly decode native-GP prose of the tested source styles even with independently secret alphabets and coefficients on all 55 pages. The fully reversible 12,956-rune secret-third-order plant and original-input gate passed [manual CI 43082068429](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43082068429). This excludes only the stated linear-feedback family and finite source register; higher tap order, within-page state changes, nonlinear stages and altered plaintext representation remain open ([proof and bounds](../im-results/lp2-pagewise-feedback-anyalphabet-capacity.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_pagewise_feedback_anyalphabet_capacity_sft.cjs)).

**Page-turn ordinal-modulus signal (exploratory, source-width-sensitive).** The signed GP-index difference at consecutive physical rune-page endpoints falls in residues **12–17 mod 29** at **23/54** page turns. The archived 2016 signed-tree JPEG is **563×569**, matching the first-row and first-column GP-prime sums of the **derived**, unprinted 11×11 reshaping of original LP2 p32's 121 runes. Their values modulo29 are **12** and **18**, retrospectively selecting precisely the previously observed 12–17 ordinal page-edge interval (**23/54**, the largest of all 29 six-residue windows). This is a source alignment discovered after the arc, not an independently specified cipher operation: ordinal *addition* gives **7/54**, GP prime-difference subtraction **8/54**, and the original 2012 503×509 source selects the neighboring 10–15 arc with **18/54**. A fixed-window deterministic 250,000-trial test gives whole-page permutation p≈**0.000316** and section-pinned p≈**0.01693**; the stricter matched-glyph nuisance controls reported below remain essential. The straightforward nine-cell Braille bit reading fails to produce prose. [Source-dimension SFT](../sims/lp2_2016_dimension_page_arc_sft.cjs) · [Reconciled page-transition study](../im-results/physical-linebreak-continuity.md). The original early/late page-disjoint window search selected 12–17 and scored **12/26** later hits (whole-page-order refit p≈**0.00467**; original-neighborhood local-displacement p≈**0.00298**). A newer original-JPEG width/section/word-status matched null reveals an important limitation: reselecting all 377 cyclic arcs on the matched early pages now picks **16–17**, yielding only **2** heldout hits, crossfit p≈**0.71549** over 15,000 matched nulls (width alone p≈**0.52103**). Holding the previously selected 12–17 arc fixed gives a weaker exact matched tail **0.03833**, with source selection uncorrected. The nulls answer different conditional questions; this is not an established page-key rule. [Integrated layout and countercheck](../im-results/physical-linebreak-continuity.md) · [Source-exact conditional crossfit](../sims/page_turn_width_word_matched_crossfit.cjs).

**Independent additive-keystream smearing bound (11 November 2026).** For \(C=P+K\pmod{29}\) with a common stationary \(\Delta K\) independent of natural-language \(\Delta P\), \(\Pr(\Delta C=0)=\sum_d\Pr(\Delta P=d)\Pr(\Delta K=-d)\geq\min_d\Pr(\Delta P=d)\). The pinned Shakespeare GP29 control has minimum delta bucket **3,778/216,873 = 1.7420%**; every one of **203,973** contiguous 12,901-transition windows has a minimum bucket of at least **168/12,901 = 1.3022%**, while original LP2 has **86/12,901 = 0.6666%** repeats. This strongly disfavors ordinary stationary independent additive OTP/Vigenère/running-key streams under an English-like plaintext model. It is a distribution-level bound, not a deterministic impossibility for a fixed key/text pair; feedback, rejection, non-additive, non-English, or plaintext-correlated page-specific schedules remain open. [Report](../im-results/lp2-anti-repeat-lossless-capacity.md#independent-additive-keystream-smearing-bound) · [SFT](../sims/lp2_independent_additive_delta_bound_sft.cjs).

**Word-internal structure.** Among **1,536 complete words of length 4–9**, only **526 nonadjacent identical-rune pairs** occur. Matched solved-LP1/ordinary-English controls usually contain substantially more. Exact consequences depend on whether source words, spelling, segmentation, and transformations are preserved. A simple position-independent deterministic rune substitution per word cannot decrease within-word equality counts. The finding is a conditional exclusion of that plaintext model. [Invariant and source controls](../im-results/wordwise-equality-invariant.md). Adjacent *word-length* dependence is also unusually small; original spaces should not automatically be treated as plaintext spaces. [Word-length study](../im-results/wordlength-order.md).

**Word-collision invariant excludes even line-static word codebooks (conditional).** All **1,645** unknown LP2 GP29 words with length ≥4 are pairwise distinct; across **2,896** printed words there are **230** equal-word pairs, all length1–3. Grouping complete words by their physical *starting* page yields **four** same-page pairs and **51/55** rune-bearing pages without any repeated word. Grouping by their **594 original printed rune lines** leaves just **one** same-line equal-word pair and **593/594** lines with no repeated word. This new source-exact key-independent constraint applies even when a deterministic word-to-word mapping has a wholly different secret codebook **on each printed line**: such maps must preserve repeated plaintext words within each line. In **2,000** original-line/word-length-matched GP-English controls for each of solved LP1+p57/Gibran/Shakespeare/Emerson, median within-line repeats are **60/57/28/45**, minima **38/34/13/24**, against one original. Under the tested ordinary-English register and original spaces-as-plaintext-spaces assumption, no book-, page- or line-static deterministic word substitution explains the manuscript. State changes *within* a printed line, changed word segmentation, unknown alternate languages and intentionally repetition-free source prose remain open. No original unknown plaintext or real key is recovered. [Full original equality partition and line controls](../im-results/lp2-word-collision-injective-source-gate.md) · [SFT](../sims/lp2_word_collision_injective_sft.cjs).

**Two-register zero-step source gate (conditional).** An exactly reversible F29 chain can assign different *unknown* source-plaintext symbols to repeated ciphertext at word beginnings and elsewhere. Searching all **841 GP marker pairs** against length-matched native-English and Latin reference words shows how source pooling can mislead: the pooled three-role Latin Vulgate assignment **G/X** looks ordinary (deviance **1.64463**, maximal 3,000-bootstrap empirical tail **0.68244**), but retaining original **word lengths 1–7/8–12** makes even the best refitted Latin **L/AE** deviance **91.20436** and gives **zero of 3,000** equally extreme source-matched nulls for **every** candidate true pair (worst plus-one tail **1/3,001**). A competing source-independent residual-repeat model, conditioned on the same 86 events across 21 word-role/length cells, fits normally (**G² 21.6042**, 100,000-null upper tail **0.4756**). A finite 128-mode authentic source-derived AES128/field28 inverse passes a full Latin-GP native control but yields no readable original plaintext; 9/12 wrong-key controls score better heldout. These are exclusions under specified plaintext-source, segmentation and key families, not a discovered Cicada cipher or a rejection of shifted plaintext boundaries. [Unified source-conditioned report and three executable controls](../im-results/dual-marker-wordrole-source-gate.md).

**Historical Stage05 fourteen-column singleton obstruction (13 November 2026).** The authenticated 2014 Stage05 transposition had **14 output columns**, numerically matching the conditional requirement of ≥14 alphabet states for 28 distinct original one-rune ciphertext glyphs if English source words are only A/I. However, granting **all 14 stationary GP29 substitution alphabets arbitrary secret permutations**, the 83 original singleton words contradict every literal 14-phase column clock examined: **12/14 global-rune phases**, **12/14 page-local phases** and **14/14 word-ordinal phases** contain ≥3 distinct observed one-rune glyphs, impossible from only A/I through a single deterministic phase map. Three complete original same-phase witnesses are p1 global350→glyph9, p2 global644→0, p5 global1316→26; for page-local phase0 they are p13 local28→1, p18 local56→16 and p54 local0→24. Full source and three 12,956-rune positive plants are in [the consolidated all-secret singleton gate](../im-results/lp2-singleton-word-cipher-state-support-bound.md#the-authentic-2014-fourteen-column-stage-cannot-be-a-stationary-fourteen-alphabet-singleton-clock) and [CJS SFT](../sims/lp2_historical14_allsecret_singleton_clock_sft.cjs). This rejects only the *stationary 14-map clock with preserved A/I words*, not a genuinely changing state, resegmentation or a new cipher; no unknown plaintext is recovered.

**Singleton-word key capacity.** The **83** original one-rune ciphertext words contain **28** distinct glyphs. Even allowing any unknown bijective 29-rune alphabet independently on each physical page, an exact all-key bound forces **13/83** to violate native A/I singleton plaintext (book-fixed: **73/83**; source-section-fixed: **57/83**). A publicly word-ordinal-periodic alphabet requires at least period **130** if book-global, **28** if section-local, or **6** if page-local to escape this *singleton-only* necessary condition; those periods are not cipher discoveries. The independently tested **previous-ciphertext-keyed** model, also allowing an arbitrary hidden GP29 permutation per observed predecessor, forces **31/82** eligible A/I singleton violations under a book-global table and **2/82** under independently section-global tables; independently page-local feedback rows escape that singleton-only bound. These exact restrictions require more state, changed segmentation or a different plaintext register under the stated same-length models. [Exact bound, controls, and scope](../im-results/word-start-reset-and-periodicity.md#exact-one-rune-word-capacity-for-arbitrary-secret-alphabets).

**Cross-word online state versus page-global postfilter (Director #590).** The 86 in-page identical-rune events include **23/2,873** original word joins and **63/10,028** original word interiors; the preheldout p0–39/p40–55 split is **17/2,141 vs 6/732** word joins, with all **32** cross-page carried words parsed as single words. An exact reversible pre-2014 GP29 pair now proves a limit on interpreting this pattern: a continuously keyed state cipher `C=(P+C_prev+1) mod29` is **ciphertext-identical for every plaintext** to independently resetting each word under `U=(P+word_length) mod29` followed by a page-global invertible output filter `C=(U-word_length+C_prev+1) mod29`. Both directions exactly replay all 12,956 original runes and recover every one of **300** source-native planted manuscripts; their ciphertext-only likelihood ratio is identically **1**, including withheld word joins. This is **structural nonidentifiability**, not evidence of either historical mechanism or recovered plaintext. This simple example reserves EA/28 for repeated outputs and is independently disfavored as a literal native-GP source by the previously established fixed-marker gate. A distinguishing decoder needs an externally prescribed postfilter or exposed intermediate stage. [Original-input theorem, heldout counts and SFT](../im-results/lp2-wordjoin-lossless-postfilter-equivalence.md) · [SFT](../sims/lp2_wordjoin_state_postfilter_equivalence_sft.cjs).

**Fixed-symbol reversible predecessor-exclusion.** A key-independent original-input test now covers **all 29** possible GP29 plaintext symbols reserved for an adjacent repeated ciphertext output, permitting arbitrary keyed 28-way bijections at every other position. The observed **86** repeat events have **23** word starts and **19** ends. Under the exact 2,896 original printed-word lengths, each of LP1, Shakespeare, Gibran and Emerson has a best-case maximum-coordinate deviation exceeding **6 SDs** after choosing the most favorable symbol and first-rune contract. The count-plausible EA and NG choices have **14 and 28** unmatched exact marker-pattern words, respectively, in the finite combined native vocabulary. All **116** full-length source-native inverse plants pass. This is a reference-conditional exclusion of **one fixed reserved plaintext symbol**, not a test of variable escapes, changing segmentation or an alternate plaintext register. [Consolidated source gate](../im-results/lp2-reserved-ea-repeat-transport-original-gate.md).

**Word-local echoes.** A **retrospective, historically earlier source derivation** now reconstructs the exact lag-six operator from the signed 2013 TCP example: BASE29 3301→3QO gives intercept24; the DH23 server public8 to generator5 fixes private exponent6; the shared secret2 supplies the candidate negative slope−2. The same prior signed example separately yields the original p39 red triad and two GF29 grid factors. **The signed challenge does not prescribe the subtraction/clock/word boundaries**, and the direct stationary six-back additive cipher is already excluded by a positive-controlled all-secret original test; this is a structural clue hypothesis only ([new source bridge](../im-results/2013-signed-dh6-original-lag6-source-bridge.md), [reproducer](../sims/lp2_signed2013_dh6_lag6_bridge_sft.cjs)). Two post-selected patterns remain measurable: **80/1,290** eligible positions satisfy the lag-six affine rule `cᵢ = 24 − 2cᵢ₋₆ (mod 29)`, and **11** lag-five positions match ᛋ to ᛋ. The manuscript also has **nine contiguous two-hit lag-five events**. An eight-section crossfit of already-selected features gains **23.272631 nats** against its constrained word-interior shuffle baseline. These properties are reproducible, though their feature discovery used the manuscript, and the gain has no discovery-wide p-value. A **lossy M3 surrogate** is not rejected by the aggregate predictor (model-matched p ≈ **0.159**); its separate clustered and S-specific counts are inadequate. Neither surrogate is a recovered reversible cipher. A **key-independent T-rotor bound** now grants a hidden permutation over all 29 GP symbols and arbitrary per-rune T exponents, even **16 independent secret alphabets** at every source-visible red-span word boundary. The printed ciphertext T-fixed symbol has global word-position counts **[4 singleton, 89 initial, 91 final, 230 interior]**; fitting all 29 hidden plaintext identities and all three GP-English source profiles gives a joint 16-section discrepancy **67.38065**, exceeding all **1,350** independent source-word-length-matched native-GP samples (largest **63.25577**). This conditionally excludes the direct native-GP **observed-T** section-static architecture. An independent scope test allowing a further hidden **post-rotor** output relabeling moves the ciphertext fixed point to any of 29 rune labels; with all 29 plaintext labels free, **17/1,050** matched native-GP controls are at least as discrepant as the original, leaving that substantially wider family unresolved. The postselected T map and English-register assumption prohibit authentic cipher or discovery-wide significance claims. [All-secret fixedpoint and red-section gate](../im-results/rotor-word-phase-attack.md#sixteen-independent-outer-alphabets-at-all-original-red-ink-starts). [Complete joint diagnostics and failure controls](../im-results/lag5-lag6-heldout-conditional-prediction.md) · [Conditional original-versus-generator test](../im-results/lag5-contiguous-echo-second-order.md).

**Bookend phrase and exact internal endpoint.** The exact adjacent 3+3-rune word pair **ᛞᛄᚢ ᛒᛖᛁ** occurs at global rune offsets **6,555–6,560** (ending at **6,561 = 3⁸ = 81²**) and the final **12,950–12,955**. It is the only repeated adjacent two-word type in the canonical unsolved text. Extending the *original ciphertext* through the first rune of independently solved p56 gives the **only repeated seven-rune window in all 13,136 original LP2 rune symbols**: `DJU-BEI-AE` at **p27:28–34** and **p55:70–75 → p56:0** (global **6,555/12,950**); no eight-rune window repeats. The last `AE` encrypts solved p56 plaintext `A`, and does not authenticate any corresponding p27 plaintext. The local starts **28/70** and page difference **28** are all multiples of seven, potentially relevant to the historical 2014 seven-column method, though the application and key are unselected. A source-calibrated uniform off-diagonal 29-rune Markov control produces **10/3,000** full-size samples with any repeated seven-rune window. An independent **word-inventory-preserving** test of the unique adjacent two-word repeat gives **4/30,000** same-or-longer repeats under visual-chapter-conditioned shuffles, versus **754/30,000** when exact per-page word inventories are held fixed; the clue's apparent rarity therefore depends strongly on whether p27/p55's two repeated word identities are treated as given. Neither conditional null is a discovery-wide probability or an authentic cipher selector. [Full source and both controls](../im-results/lp2-bookend7-p56-known-boundary.md) · [word-inventory SFT](../sims/lp2_bookend_wordpair_layout_null_sft.cjs). The first three physical LP2 pages contain **729 = 3⁶** runes, and the suffix after 6,561 has **6,395 = 80²−5** runes. These retrospectively observed square counts propose a *hypothetical* 81×81/80×80 framing; they do not establish a cipher reset. The five actually printed red Arabic numerals on pp36–38 give source-fixed insertion positions completing the 80×80 square. A [128-variant reversible square arithmetic test](../im-results/bookend-81x81-80x80-ordinal-insertion.md) recovers no native-GP plaintext. Independently, the [full original-source ECC200 four-phase alphabet closure](../im-results/lp2-datamatrix-five-ordinal-reed-solomon-closure.md) forces all **116** unknown binary phase/rune assignments from **1,216** unaltered finder modules and rejects all **1,024** five-bit completions by exact Reed–Solomon syndromes (at least **187/192** nonzero, planted valid symbol **0**; [successful CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/42942931537)). These exclusions do not authenticate the square framing. A separate source-fixed **lag-6,395 bookend state-reuse gate** tests the exact repeated 3+3-rune pair at offsets 6,555 and 12,950 as a direct reset anchor. Under exact reuse of one additive GP29 pad, lagged ciphertext equality is key-independent; the original has only **199/6,561** equal pairs, while **0/6,000** deterministic 12,956-rune Shakespeare/Gibran/Emerson control windows are that low (source minima **324/363/357**). A literal repeated ciphertext-step stream is also impossible: only **255/6,560** lag-paired steps match, versus 6,560 required by exact state repetition. This closes those two simplest reset readings without selecting a key; plaintext-dependent rejection/key-skip state and other nonlinear mechanisms remain open ([report](../im-results/lp2-bookend-6395-sharedpad-gate.md); [SFT](../sims/lp2_bookend_6395_sharedpad_sft.cjs)). Original-source Fourier constraints rule against arbitrary separable GP-English row/column additive pads in these layouts. A further exact **all-secret-alphabet cross-square bound** permits each of the source square, second-block ciphertext and decoded output to have its own unrestricted 29-symbol alphabet permutation: over all 256 crop/orientation pairings, the highest possible count of any one resulting GP rune is **395/6,395**. All 169 matched-length Shakespeare/Gibran GP-English source windows contain at least **657** E runes; thus such a direct combined-source cipher needs at least 262 additional symbol alterations before it could resemble even the weakest of these native-GP controls. The 6,395-rune synthetic planted key is exactly recovered. This is a source-original conditional exclusion, with no authenticated square boundary or historical key. A separate **reversible Fibonacci bilinear rank-one cipher**, allowing each square column an unrestricted secret key, also fails: Fibonacci modulo29 has exact period14, forcing five or more *unmasked* GP plaintext rows for every key and phase. Its decoder recovers both blinded original-length Shakespeare controls exactly (12,956/12,956 GP runes) but cannot produce source-like heldout original plaintext. More decisively, **all 16-row-periodic, column-specific secret 29-symbol permutation ciphers** preserve every rune-equality pair 16 square rows apart, independent of all 1,296/1,280 arbitrary hidden alphabet keys. Authentic LP2 has only **151/5,265** such row-pair equalities in the 81² block and **178/5,115** in the terminal block; independent GP-literary windows have minima **265 and 267** under the same geometric tests. These are conditional exclusions of two candidate cipher families, not recoveries or proof of the retrospective square layout. A proposed **6,395-rune synchronized key recurrence** also failed. The source-suggested **179-/1,432-word key clock** also fails under arbitrary bijective per-word-position substitutions: LP2 has only **303/8,717** and **154/4,691** same-position equalities, while 2,000 repeat-repaired native-GP plants per reference have minima **423** and **210** (zero at/below LP2). This rejects only those periodic bijective word-position registers; nonlinear/nonperiodic states and other plaintext registers remain open. A new [source-original nonlinear stencil bound](../im-results/lp2-repeated-wordpair-structure.md) exhausts 8 orientations, 15 local 2×2 corner masks and every placement of a contiguous five-cell omission: even permitting two secret lookup tables and 86 arbitrary changes, any stationary transform depending on at most three source corners requires **at least 378 edits**. A separate **all-gap GF29 polynomial rank witness** excludes every full-four-corner source transform of total degree≤3 even with **165** alterations and completely independent secret polynomial keys per 36-rune block. A fully unrestricted four-corner map with arbitrary non-collision changes remains open. A separate **exact, key-independent predecessor-collision intersection gate** rejects *all* stationary arbitrary four-corner tables that redraw only when their first tentative output equals the preceding ciphertext rune: at least **10 contradictory source tuples** survive every isometry and five-cell gap, even granting independent secret tables on either side. The square framing remains hypothetical. These exclusions concern specific assumed square layouts and plaintext registers, not a discovered 6,561 cipher reset. [Phrase, conditional nulls, source-square tests](../im-results/lp2-repeated-wordpair-structure.md) · [Fibonacci bilinear source inverse](../im-results/fibonacci-bilinear-square-inversion.md) · [Recurrence exclusion](../im-results/bookend-repeat-lag6395.md) · [Bookend period-179/1432 word-position gate](../im-results/2016-28page-bookend-ratio-inverse.md#period-179-and-period-1432-word-position-substitution-gate).

**Marginal dependence and diagonal-only repeat suppression.** Frequencies are close to uniform over 29 runes. The original stream has **86/12,901** within-page equal adjacent pairs (**0.6666%**) versus **445.77** expected (**3.4553%**) under an IID null using the observed unigram frequencies; the overlap-corrected diagnostic is **z = −17.3409**. The deficit is confined to the diagonal: off-diagonal ordered-pair counts remain close to the unigram-conditioned null. The one-parameter fitted repeat-survival factor **q = 86/445.77 ≈ 0.19293** predicts **86.00** equal pairs by construction; the separate, source-suggested one-fifth factor **q = 0.20** predicts **89.15**. Neither rate identifies a cipher. A separate all-page concatenation (including eight inter-section edges) splits the 86 pairs into **63/10,060 within words** and **23/2,895 across word boundaries**; **4/593** cross printed-line markers versus **82/12,362** within lines, and **0/54** repeats at physical page joins. Under the nine-section convention used by Wulfic, omit the eight inter-section edges: the same 86 pairs are **63/10,060** and **23/2,887** over **12,947** transitions, with **4/585** line-break and **0/48** page-break equalities. None of the eight excluded section joins repeats. Fisher exact tests show no detectable rate difference (word vs interword **p=0.3625**; line vs within-line **p=0.7988**), so the deficit is not supported as a per-word or per-line reset. A source-exact key-skip versus output-rewrite gate finds the two geometric retry models ciphertext-equivalent (likelihood ratio **1** for every sequence); a fixed page-heldout check cannot distinguish them, and the pinned external Round 18 battery measured separation power **0.175**. Neither model supplies an LP2 key or recoverable side state. This leaves the deficit compatible with a global soft filter or another stream-level correlation, not an identified cipher or proof that doublets are control symbols ([model gate](../im-results/lp2-anti-repeat-lossless-capacity.md#key-skip-and-output-rewrite-are-not-identifiable-from-ciphertext)). A concrete external proposal is now closed as non-reversible: Wulfic's pinned encrypt_dodging and ideal key-switch generators have fixed-key/fixed-seed plaintext collisions, while its C14 skip-next test reports LLR −21.34 (exclusion threshold −10) and its L5 DIVINITY/CIRCUMFERENCES switch yields only 6/2,900 or 10/2,898 equalities. See the [exact reproduction and model gate](../im-results/lp2-anti-repeat-lossless-capacity.md#wulfic-s-19-anti-doublet-candidate-is-not-a-reversible-rule). This excludes those specified generators, not all stateful anti-repeat ciphers. A corrected coordinate audit finds one page-initial doublet, p51 `ᚹᚹ` at local offsets 0–1 (book offsets **12,114–12,115**), immediately after the 10-dot/table bridge; the earlier zero-page-start assertion was a counting bug. Neither near-uniformity nor scarcity of doublets identifies a cipher. A source-gated hard-no-repeat digraph follow-up rejects the natural pairings: continuous phase puts at least **37/86** equal pairs inside digraphs; even an independently optimal phase per page leaves **17 contradictions across 14 pages**; standard word-start pairing puts **27/63** within-word repeats inside a Playfair digraph. A free phase per word is retrospective; no source selects a 29-symbol square, pair phase/reset, key or filler rule. Retire these hard-no-repeat polygraphic readings; this does not exclude evolving/non-bijective filters ([report](../im-results/lp2-polygraphic-pair-alignment-gate.md); [boundary reproducer](../sims/lp2_global_doublet_boundary_audit.cjs); [SFT](../sims/lp2_polygraphic_pair_alignment_gate.cjs)). A source-motivated stationary five-state GP29 substitution is also excluded by exact within-phase collision invariants: book/page/13-dot clocks give original rates 0.03448–0.03453 versus native-control minima ≥0.05663 across 2,335 windows each; five clause-specific alphabets on the 553-rune red section give 0.034906 versus native minimum 0.051704 across 2,526 windows. Independent secret-permutation plants recover exactly; evolving or non-bijective filters remain open. [Five-phase SFT](../sims/lp2_five_phase_allsecret_sft.cjs) · [audit](../im-results/agent48-nongp-register-audit.md). [Full audit](../im-results/agent48-nongp-register-audit.md) · [reproducer](../sims/state_class_nongp_register_audit.cjs) · [external prior-art measured ceiling](https://github.com/Dukotah/cicada3301/blob/master/liber-primus/MEASURED-CEILING.md). #505's source-gated audit of the signature-bearing 2012, 2013, 2014 and 2016 materials found no LP2 variable-length/fractionated transformation or delimiter grammar; BASE29 is authenticated only as a numeral conversion within the 2013 TCP protocol, not as a GP-word codec. No fractionation family is source-selected (bounded no-selector result).


**Source-visible escape-channel capacity for the 29→28 no-repeat hypothesis (12 November 2026).** The observed 86 repeats are not an authenticated encoder rule. Conditional injectivity bounds are **325.450 bits** if up to 86 actual repeats may occur at freely chosen positions, **648.770 bits** (649-bit side channel) if 86 exception positions are fixed and allow all 29 outputs, **430.561–445.732 bits** under per-page repeat-count constraints, and **1,066.559 bits** if the observed repeated outputs are forced at a fixed mask. A mixed-radix synthetic codec round-trips arbitrary 12,902-symbol GP29 inputs with the fixed-exception 649-bit side channel and no ordinary adjacent repeats; this proves the bound tight, not an LP2 decryption. The 55 rune-bearing pages contain **2,873** explicit within-page typographic delimiter gaps; their fixed-count boundary-position space has an upper capacity of **9,638.332 bits** (at least **50.730 bits on every page**), so word-boundary placement could carry enough information in principle. Red/special colour and mark choices alone have an intentionally overgenerous ceiling of **275 bits** (78 bits at red-span block level), below the most permissive 325.450-bit deficit. Therefore there is **no capacity contradiction** if word-boundary positions are allowed as an escape channel, but no source-selected reversible plaintext-to-boundary mapping has been identified. This is only a combinatorial ceiling, not evidence of a cipher or recovered text ([full per-page audit and planted codec](../im-results/lp2-no-repeat-source-visible-channel-capacity.md); [reproducer](../sims/lp2_no_repeat_escape_channel_capacity_sft.cjs)).

## 3. Clue-linked candidates: genuinely open, with bounded direct failures

**Historical 2013 BASE29 example identifies an exact original-grid quadratic.** A preserved 2013 Cicada server-challenge excerpt explicitly gives `BASE29 3301 → 3QO`, the base29 digits `[3,26,24]`. Reading those digits with the same ascending-power polynomial convention as the actual LP2 red triads gives `3+26X+24X² = 24(X²+18X+11)` in F29, **exactly one pre-existing irreducible quadratic factor** of the original 256-byte LP2 table polynomial. The **other** 2013 protocol example, `DH 23` with generator5, server share8, client share19 and common key2, has client private exponent **15**. Consequently `15(X−(3301 mod29))² = 27+5X+15X²`, **exactly the independent p39 red triad factor**; its constant `27=−2` and linear coefficient `5` agree with the worked exchange. The same two exact factor constraints also resolve archival transcription errors: of ten 2014 `sexagesimal.bin` versus original-LP2 byte discrepancies, independently sourced case/correction alternatives at **nine** non-mod29-congruent positions have only **one** jointly divisible assignment among 512, exactly restoring those nine printed values. **Authenticated 2016 continuation clue.** The archived `4gq25` image and OutGuess payload are preserved byte-for-byte at [the original 563×569 JPEG](../resources/historical/cicada2016-4gq25.jpg) and [the 1,136-byte clear-signed artifact](../resources/historical/cicada2016-4gq25-outguess.asc); both Git blob SHAs match the external archive, and GnuPG independently reports a **Good signature** against the repository's Cicada public key (fingerprint `6D85 4CD7 9333 22A6 01C3 286D 181F 01E5 7A35 090F`; GnuPG trust remains `[unknown]`). The authenticated instruction is: **“Liber Primus is the way. Its words are the map, their meaning is the road, and their numbers are the direction.”** The message also warns, “Beware false paths.” A contemporaneous 19 January 2016 forum repost preserves the same signature and normalized signed body: the two internal double spaces after “way.” and “paths.” are authenticated but consistent with ordinary two-space sentence spacing, while the trailing space after “their” in the OutGuess extraction is unsigned and absent from the repost. No byte-derived LP2 key follows ([whitespace audit](../im-results/2016-signed-payload-whitespace-audit.md)). The signature-verified 2013 first-onion `clue.txt.asc` predates LP2 and says “You can't see the forest when you're looking at the trees”; its signed next-stage `hello.txt.asc` supplies only the next onion address, not a tree traversal ([source audit, Issue #113](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/113)). The signature-verified 2013 forest clue now motivates a separate aggregate-block hypothesis: the identical botanical-border span on physical pp40–55 (archive JPEGs57–72), 16 images containing 15 rune-bearing pages plus numeric-only p50. Those 15 text pages (p40–49 and p51–55) contain 3,316 runes = 3,301 + 15. This is a candidate block checksum, not a key. The apparent 810 = 45 × 18 relation is conditional: 45 is the 2016 signed body's whitespace-token count (43 natural-language words plus 3301 and 7A35090F), 18 is 569 mod29 from the 563×569 carrier, and 810 counts raw whitespace/hyphen fragments—not canonical words (735 logical words; 747 slash-normalized fragments). The canonical 810-word window is elsewhere (segments 30–44, 3,656 runes); no 15-page window combines 3,316 runes with 810 logical words. The direct 45×18 mod-18 selector using GP-prime sums for words and decimal/hex values for the numeric tokens fails in both natural matrix orientations and both index bases. A second exploratory 16×16 page-key schedule, formed by reshaping the 256-cell p49–p51 grid row-major and assigning one 16-byte row to each p40–p55 page, also fails: both candidate radices and C±K remain near-uniform, with no plaintext. The 16×16 mapping is not source-prescribed ([report](../im-results/2013-forest-2016-tree-border-aggregate-gate.md); [SFT](../sims/forest_45x18_selector_sft.cjs)). This block-level interpretation does not overturn the negative p32 tree-topology result: the clue still fixes no root, start, order, direction or endpoint for a p32 traversal. The 2016 delivery audit also finds no separate authenticated service response: the documented chain is tweet → static JPEG → OutGuess → signed payload. The two public mirrors contain only the stage01/2016 README, image and extracted payload (no HTML, stage02 or server files); the contemporaneous thread's cicada3301.org/liber and 52.32.251.99 links are unsigned participant posts, not authenticated endpoints. No signed/raw HTTP or HTML response or server capture survives in the reviewed sources, so the signed map sentence supplies no further route grammar ([source gate](../im-results/lp2-tree121-signed2016-source-gate.md)). A frozen 29q+r line:character candidate on the signed words yields L C B P P R NG NG P A R R TH B P C and isolates P R NG (PRNG) inside “map their meaning”; the full 43-word output is incoherent. The readout requires four unselected choices (using signed-message words as inputs, longest-GP transliteration, dropping separator slots, and remainder-as-line/quotient-as-rune). No historical book-code source selects this composite grammar or a PRNG seed, so retire this exact selector without excluding other PRNG ciphers ([source gate](../im-results/2016-signed-prng-linechar-source-gate.md); [SFT](../sims/2016_signed_prng_linechar_gate_sft.cjs)). The extracted payload SHA-256 is `fe89e77519dfaca998a257ed39c2861303029de2a49183db00eea0fda3a28010`. **P32 nested Möbius chain (exact algebra on a derived reshape; no key or plaintext).** The central 5×5 perimeter of the **derived** 11×11 GP-prime square has 16 symbols summing to **1,136**, exactly the archived 2016 ASCII-signed file length, though that armor length is not itself authenticated by the PGP signature. Two source-readable matrix conventions arise. Counting each ring cell once, with the complete top/bottom rows owning the four corners, gives N/E/S/W sums **[275,257,373,231]** and \(M_7=[[14,25],[25,28]]\), projective order **7**. Summing all five cells on each inclusive side gives **[275,347,373,339]** and \(C=[[14,28],[25,20]]\), projective order **28**. With \(S=[[11,8],[0,1]]\), whose entries are the outer side length 11 and half the ring length 8, the two source matrices satisfy \(M_7\sim SC^4S^{-1}\); the exponent 4 is the number of sides. Thus the two readings form a nested map, not unrelated alternatives. A separate original-image audit of the repeated Möbius/infinity mark on pp0–2 and p15 finds all seven instances share the same orientation and crossing/gap pattern; no unique entry, exit, seam or handedness is marked, and 729=27² does not select a 27×27 fill order. A finite historical cipher-disk survey (#469) found no pre-2014 rotating-alphabet device whose specific geometry and visible state rules are selected by the mark; ordinary infinity notation (Wallis 1655) remains a noncryptographic fit. No rotor test was run. The opening motif is therefore not an executable traversal selector; the algebraic p32 nested chain remains source algebra, not a key or plaintext ([visual audit](../im-results/original-visual-clue-operation-map.md); [original-image SFT](../sims/lp2_mobius_motif_geometry_sft.py)).

The LP1 p05 corner matrix \(A=[[11,6],[6,11]]\) and p16 matrix \(B=[[28,9],[9,28]]=21A^6\) complete a cross-release chain. The inclusive map satisfies \(C\sim Q_{28}A^3Q_{28}^{-1}\) for \(Q_{28}=[[17,8],[0,1]]\), i.e. \(x\mapsto8-12x\): 12 is the signed image width 563 mod29 and 8 is half the 16-cell ring. Composing \(Q_7=SQ_{28}=[[13,9],[0,1]]\) gives \(M_7\sim Q_7A^{12}Q_7^{-1}\); here 13 is the p32 red 13-dot mark, 9 is half the image-height residue \(569\bmod29=18\), and \(12=3\) peeled layers \(\times4\) sides \(=563\bmod29\). A reflection-equivalent representation is \(M_7\sim Q_2A^{16}Q_2^{-1}\) with \(Q_2=[[16,9],[0,1]]\). A bounded search over the source-derived affine values and all 28 powers finds only these two orientation-equivalent parameterizations for each map; this is not exhaustive over all projective transformations.

The row-major map's fixed points **22 and 25** have GP-prime weights **83+101=184**, matching the solved p57 first body word **LIKE** under GP normalization \(L,I,C,E\) (K→C). The original p32 JPEG's 13 red dots lie in nine rows [1,2,1,2,1,2,1,2,1] and columns [4,5,4]. An independent cross-page source-JPEG control finds this **identical 5+8 stencil** on p53 and two solved-p56 punctuation marks, with centered dot positions within **0.71 pixel**. The repeated stencil is not unique by location, but its p32 row code supplies a second source-derived selector candidate: single-dot rows→1 and double-dot rows→0 give `101010101₂=341`; the complement is `010101010₂=170`; modulo29 these are `{22,25}`, exactly M7's projective fixed points. Exhaustive enumeration of all 16 corner allocations finds only the natural top/bottom-own-corners assignment with this pair. A 200,000-shuffle source-preserving null (first row/column fixed; other 100 p32 prime values permuted) gives 632/200,000 matches for the pair plus order7 when any assignment is allowed (add-one conditional frequency 0.003165), 3,104/200,000 for the pair alone (0.015525), and 224/200,000 for the fixed horizontal assignment (0.001125). The same 5+8 stencil recurs on p53 and two solved-p56 marks, and the binary coding is not explicitly prescribed; treat this as cross-page selector evidence, not proof of intent ([geometry SFT](../sims/p32_red13_mark_geometry_sft.py); [selector SFT](../sims/p32_redmark_fixedpoint_selector_sft.cjs); [cross-page control](../sims/lp2_marker_template_source_sft.py)).

A new exact **source-count self-description** strengthens this orientation. P32 has **22 page-local segments but 21 word starts** (segment 0 is the incoming one-rune carry from p31), its central 5×5 has **25 cells**, and its first punctuated clause has **7 page-local segments** (one carry plus six complete word starts). In the ordered fixed-point coordinate `h(x)=(x−22)/(x−25)`, M7 acts as multiplication by 7. The counts 22/25/7 therefore reconstruct the projective representative `[[8,6],[6,16]]`; multiplying by the mark's **9 occupied rows** gives the exact independently derived ring representative `[[14,25],[25,28]]`. More specifically, 22 is the only fixed point that is a valid p32 page-local segment address. The **22nd page-local segment (the 21st complete word start)** `ᚪᚷᛒᛇ` has GP-prime sum **216 = 7×29+13**, independently reproducing both the multiplier 7 and the mark's 13-dot seed. M7 then sends **13→24 = 3301 mod29**. Under the same 200,000 source-preserving shuffle, the ordered map signature occurs **108** times for any legal corner assignment and **6** times for the original horizontal assignment; exact segment-22 sum 216 occurs **2,064** times; the joint conditions leave **3** any-assignment matches and **0** horizontal matches, giving add-one conditional frequencies about **0.000020** and **0.000005**. These are post-discovery conditional frequencies, and GP-prime sum as the signed clue's “number” remains interpretive. The chain strengthens M7 as a source structural map while still yielding no key or plaintext ([consolidated derivation](../im-results/tree-prime-canvas-partition.md#the-p32-source-counts-reconstruct-the-selected-order-seven-map); [updated SFT](../sims/p32_redmark_fixedpoint_selector_sft.cjs)). The map also sends the p32 13-dot count to **24**, exactly `3301 mod29`. A stronger adjacent-page check uses the two punctuation-selected clause-ending words: page-local segment 7 (a complete word start) has GP-prime sum **14**, while segment 22 (the 21st complete word start) has sum **216 ≡ 13 mod29**. The source M7 maps their ordered residues **[14,13]→[18,24]**, exactly the ordered residues of the immediately following p33 cuneiform values **1033** and **3301**. Across all 16 legal corner assignments, only the same horizontal mask 4 produces this ordered mapping; inverse action and one-sided order reversals fail. In the 200,000 source-preserving shuffle, the full count-self-description plus the clause-end→cuneiform mapping occurs **0** times under either the fixed horizontal rule or any legal corner assignment, for add-one conditional frequency **1/200,001 ≈ 0.000005**. This is post-discovery cross-artifact verification rather than plaintext recovery. The cuneiform digits also reconstruct the projectivity independently: `17→13`, `13→24` (`3301 mod29`), and `18→26` (`1033 mod29→55 mod29`) determine the unique normalized map `[[15,4],[4,1]]`, projectively identical to M7. Scaling it by **16 = rank(3301)/29** gives `[[8,6],[6,16]]`, exactly the independent 22/25/7 count-derived representative; its entries also equal `[[464/58,174/29],[174/29,464/29]]`. This supplies a third source route to the same PGL₂ class, while the quotient arrangement remains post-discovery structural evidence. Its seven-cycle through 13 is \(13\to24\to19\to3\to6\to20\to17\to13\); the six finite states in its infinity cycle have prime weights summing **174=6×29**, also the prime-weight sum of the inclusive map's fixed points 20 and 25. The exact 200,000-draw conditional null, preserving the 21 first-row/first-column values and permuting the other 100 source primes, found the nested \(M_7\sim SC^4S^{-1}\) identity in **13/200,000** draws for the fixed horizontal-row rule and **126/200,000** when any of 16 corner assignments was allowed (135 matching assignments total). These post-selected conditional frequencies are not discovery-wide p-values. A separate inclusive-map null found 16/200,000 for its combined characteristic/width-root-gap condition and none also matching the 1,136 ring sum.

The complete source-seeded 28-cycle/EA differential inverse, direct 7-cycle substitution/state inverses, direct pairwise M7 Hill interpretation, and the exact M7 orbit as a period-7 additive GP29 keystream on p33–39 (both physical and red-title starts, signs and directions; familywise page-rotation p=0.947) remain ciphertext-like; no new plaintext. The p15 4×4 table suggests the four-rune edge length; HOME at offset33 and BUDS at offset93 form the observed +60 pair. The four interior diagonal prime weights sum 318=2838 mod360. The ray is therefore not a supported route or destination. Direct p55 additive/Hill/transposition readouts remain negative.  The separate M7×p50 nonlinear composition on the 10,240-rune complement (40×256) was tested over 16 fixed models: M7 or M7⁻¹ after c±(p50 table mod29), both target orders and both table orders, with infinity rendered as a separator. The 174/464 prime ranks and 29/58 quotients are mathematical, not selected LP2 operations; p33 does not select a p50 base60 codec (#440 closed).

**2016 signed-image dimension prime starts and all-secret state bound.** The authenticated 2016 image's **563×569** pixel dimensions are consecutive ordinary primes **p103 and p104**. Transferring the genuinely solved LP2 p56 consecutive-prime totient algorithm to original unsolved p0 with either dimension as its first prime, while exhausting **all 16,384 literal-F masks** for each, yields only GP-trigram scores **−6.13356** and **−6.15849/rune**, versus solved p56's independently authenticated **−2.38385/rune**; no unknown plaintext. If instead the two primes define a modular-square state **N=563×569=320,347**, CRT forces every possible secret starting state to enter a squaring period dividing **70** after at most three steps, verified exhaustively for **all 320,347 seeds**. Any unchanged bijective GP29 output map indexed only by that state preserves lag-70 ciphertext/plaintext equality. Original LP2 has **433/12,883** such book-global equalities, below the minimum **693** across **514,817** contiguous equal-length Shakespeare/Emerson GP-reference windows; page-local resets also fail **293/8,948** versus all 2,000 matched windows (minimum469). A correctly encrypted native-GP plant recovers **12,956/12,956**. This excludes two precise, source-motivated direct native-GP mechanisms and does not show that the image dimensions were intended cipher parameters; evolving/rekeyed states and non-GP transports remain open. **p32 prime-ordinal EN printed-word correspondence (exact arithmetic; no source-selected inverse; #492 closed).** The original signed 2016 tree JPEG measures 563×569 (ordinary primes #103/#104), and those primes equal the first-row/first-column GP-prime sums of LP2 p32's 11×11 rune matrix. The corresponding **one-based** rune positions 103–104 spell the **complete source-printed word EN**, followed by another word beginning NW. Across all 2,896 canonical unknown words, 16 complete words self-point by that formula. The zero-based **NN** pair crosses the actual EN/NW word boundary and remains a retired off-by-one north cue. This arithmetic return is exact, while the EN route does not land on a complete word start; obtaining BUDS and using its totient as an address are unprescribed steps. The 2014 Wisdom source's general prime/totient language and solved p56's `φ(prime_j)=prime_j−1` keystream do not select `φ(96)` on a composite word sum or a page-pointer use. **No primary source selects the composite prime-rank→word→totient operation or an executable next step** (#492 CLOSED); retain the exact correspondence as a conditional cross-artifact checksum, not a plaintext recovery or decryption key. **2013 five-of-ten SSSS transfer to LP2 remains unestablished.** The reconstructed pklmx stage genuinely used a 5-of-10 SSSS, but no LP2 share payload or x/y mapping is source-identified. **Historical Parable-prime source: all-secret-state period restriction.** The original TXXX field in Cicada's 2013 `AUDIO/761.MP3` is the product checksum of the solved *PARABLE* body-clause prime sums described above. The following theorem bounds only the separate hypothesis that this full number is used as a modular-squaring keystream modulus; the metadata relationship does not establish that cipher. If the full number were used as a modular-squaring keystream modulus, a CRT group-order theorem forces **every possible secret starting state** to become periodic within **1,224 square steps** after at most two initial steps; an eight-parity-bit-per-rune stream would have a 153-rune period. Original unsolved LP2 has **416/12,801 = 0.03250** same-rune matches at lag153 after excluding the two transient positions, versus **0.06038** in independently sampled English GP control. Transparent-EA field-step ranks also lack the English equality signal. A complete **5,201,280-candidate** two-prime start search, an additional **2,600,640-candidate** hidden keyskip screen, and **29,459,892** direct historical decoded-waveform sample-offset tests all failed to recover unknown text while passing source-native concealed-key English recovery tests. The exact theorem restricts **only direct synchronized additive/field-step native-GP output**. Hidden state advance, non-English intermediates, distinct cipher families and external continuations remain open. Source-fixed 28-/7-period keys, a factor-root recurrence, seed-24 feedback, and 3×3 Hill candidates were tested (196 variants, plus 16 terminal-red variants) without plaintext. Best full-stream GP trigram is −11.04887 versus raw −11.07314 and solved-reference holdout −8.41667; IoC remains uniform-like. This is a candidate cross-round clue, not an authenticated key ([report](../im-results/2013-audio-mod29-crossround-key.md); [reproducer](../sims/parable_mod29_crossround_key_probe.cjs)). **Collision-only hidden consumption:** the fixed 7-/28-period keys were also tested with a reversible periodic Viterbi that permits a skip only when the skipped key would emit the previous ciphertext rune. A 1,200-symbol English plant recovered exactly; the source key orders ranked at the 20th/30th percentiles against 20 random cyclic-order controls, with no LP2 plaintext ([report](../im-results/2013-audio-mod29-collision-viterbi.md); [reproducer](../sims/parable_mod29_periodic_collision_viterbi.cjs)).  A separate prime-ordinal square from the same 2013 audio yields candidate B=[[1,7],[25,10]] and the exact p3/p39 conjugacy M0^6S=SB, B^5=11I. A separate candidate three-prime textbook-RSA interpretation of the same Parable product was bounded. The p50 base60 table has only 19/64 big-endian or 32/64 little-endian four-byte blocks below N. Its alternative 85-block 3-byte framing decrypts to 595 nonlinguistic GP29 symbols under all three tested exponents: e=65,537 (documented 2014 Cicada RSA stage; current Emerson-GP score −6.2661), e=761 (2013 MP3 basename/TIT2 title sum; −6.4733), and e=3,301 (2013 TPE1 performer; −6.3120). The latter two are source-derived candidates, not authenticated exponent instructions; low-digit matches to solved p56 are 0/85, 2/85 and 5/85, respectively. The previously reported −5.9354 score for e=65,537 is not reproduced by the current checked-in scorer/control. No fixed seven-rune LP2 block phase can satisfy the leading-digit bound 0–2 because every phase contains all 29 runes. This rejects only these direct textbook-RSA framings, not other uses of the Parable number ([report](../im-results/audio-761-pad-scan.md#candidate-three-prime-textbook-rsa-interpretation-bounded-negative); [reproducer](../sims/parable_three_prime_rsa_grid_sft.cjs)). Retire further waveform-derived LP2 key searches pending new primary evidence.
