**79/78 packet base conversion: one conditional survivor.** A previously documented original-image/source-text enclosure puts five visibly red numbered paragraphs between two ten-dot punctuation marks, spanning **553 = 7×79** ciphertext runes at global [8,805, 9,358); each immediately preceding original rune occupies **35 mod79**. At the eight corresponding 79-separated positions, the GP runes spell **HUAAUAGC**, whose central **UAA UAG** are two RNA stop codons. This is the only adjacent RNA-stop-codon pair at stride79 across all global phases; under 10,000 full-book physical-page circular rotations, only **3/10,000** had any such pair (conditional add-one **4/10,001**). The 79-phase, enclosure and biological reading were recognized retrospectively; the countercheck does **not** calibrate the full discovery search, identify a key, or remove the enclosure's one ciphertext doublet. The 79-step register also has only **23/164 RNA-base symbols** and **no AUG start codon**; its seven-rune base run cannot be translated into a starting message. More decisively, the apparent **UAA** crosses red headings 8,944/9,035 and physical p36→37; the apparent **UAG** crosses headings 9,133/9,229 and physical p37→38. Neither stop begins at an original heading, page edge, or ten-dot marker. An exact original-only all-alphabet census additionally finds that **A/U/G/C** is **one of only 3/23,751 four-symbol sets** yielding seven consecutive symbols in the source-fixed phase35 79-step stream (versus **273/23,751** when searching all 79 phases); the other 78 source phases max out at five literal biological bases. This conditionally strengthens the possibility of a *deliberately placed four-base clue* and warrants preserving the geometry, but establishes no codon frame, key or unknown plaintext. This **closes the literal RNA-terminator decipherment route** (#577), while leaving the source-visible seven-by-79 enclosure intact. [Passed original-source workflow 42882077960](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/42882077960). [Source-only replication and scope](../im-results/prime79-radix29-packet-gate.md#the-original-five-numbered-paragraphs-form-seven-exact-79-rune-clock-intervals). The source has exactly **12,956 = 164×79** unsolved GP runes. For the explicit reversible hypothesis that 78 base29 plaintext digits become 79 base28 digits under a single unknown 29-rune cyclic order and a global packet clock, exhaustive first- and second-digit numerical-capacity constraints exclude **157/158** phase/orientation variants. The sole **forward phase35** candidate numerically inverts **95/95** repeat-free packets (7,410 candidate GP runes), but its rune IoC is **0.03444**, essentially uniform, and the original 86 repeat outputs remain unexplained. A 3,000-order first-half-trained/second-half-tested language search also returned near-uniform outputs. An exact global all-permutation source-language bound, calibrated against **1,096 actual known plaintext** GP runes (excluding encrypted p56), limits the **95 packet-leading GP runes** to at most **40** of the eight most common symbols (59.03% in native controls), and limits their optimistic 95-rune GP-unigram log score to **−344.3935**. Every corresponding 95-start window from **217,644 Shakespeare** and **62,696 Gibran** GP-control runes scored higher than **−314.61**, strongly disfavoring this numerical survivor as a **direct GP-prose encoding**. A stronger **directed-cycle capacity proof** now excludes **every unrestricted fixed-width binary integer packet** within this exact 78-base29-to-79-base28 transport: at most 378 input bits force leading steps 1–9, yet all 158 original phase/orientation cases exhibit forbidden directed three-cycles under any secret cyclic rune order; all fixed sorted orders likewise violate a ten-leader bound. Inputs of 379 or more unconstrained bits exceed the 78-digit base29 capacity. The 316 finite fixed-order variants are excluded with synthetic recovery controls. An additional **all-secret globally salted binary-packet proof** allows any single additive offset before radix28 conversion: the leading packet digit must occupy one unknown **ten-rank circular window**. For the phase35 original clock in **both orientations**, all **56/56** ten-rank window × direction cases are exact **UNSAT** against every secret cyclic 29-rune order (95 source-intact packets per direction, 84/92 distinct leading edges). Even **eleven-rank** arcs are excluded in **56/56** original cases, yielding an exact lower bound on required globally bounded source-value span. A **58-edge source-original certificate** suffices for the forward ten-rank exclusion; four full-size hidden-order integer plants invert exactly. The sorted-global-order model is likewise excluded for **all 158 phases/directions**, since its unknown ten-rank window admits at most 12 distinct leading glyphs versus the source minimum 25. This remains conditional on one global salt and fixed alphabet; other offset clocks under a cyclic secret order and per-packet salts are not excluded. [Exact source-original proof and salted generalization](../im-results/prime79-binary-capacity-cycle.md) · [Global-salt CSP with native controls](../sims/prime79_global_salt_cycle_csp.py). Variable framing, restricted codebooks and other binary intermediates remain open. An exact **GF29 terminal-symbol rank proof** excludes a fixed last GP rune per book, section, physical page or periodic packet clock of length 1–79, **and any affine combination of 18 source-fixed grid, red-rune, prime and page-number clocks**, for every possible secret rune order under that surviving alignment; a blind native-GP fixed-tail plant recovers its true secret order, 7,410 plaintext runes and 7,505 ciphertext runes. A further key-independent [95,28] GF29 terminal-parity code excludes **all 568,405 contiguous Shakespeare, Gibran and Emerson source-text windows** under this exact packet clock and GP tokenizer, even allowing **two unknown altered packet-terminal runes**; a synthetic source-aligned hidden-order inverse exactly re-encrypts 7,505 clean ciphertext runes. This is a finite three-reference exclusion and does not rule out other texts or enciphering architectures. Arbitrary unpadded packet contents remain open. A separate top-k gate tests the postselected 164×79/78-phase rank model as two direct base28 byte registers (4→2 and 5→3): all **2,592** declared clock/mix, segmentation, marker, orientation and offset cases fail even with 78 arbitrary fixed secret phase permutations. The 4→2 codec forces at least **2,269/2,871** invalid leading bytes in its most permissive case; the 5→3 codec forces at least **281/2,216** non-seven-bit leading bytes. The natural payload78/no-salt continuous cases force **2,557/3,176** and **392/2,541**, respectively. Both planted ASCII controls round-trip exactly. This excludes only these fixed-phase byte registers, not salt×column/per-packet rekeys, larger/variable packings or other binary encodings. [Exact bounds and positive controls](../im-results/field79-phase78-base28-ascii-gate.md) · [Reproducer](../sims/field79_phase78_base28_ascii_gate.cjs). A distinct [source-original arbitrary-coefficient GF29 parity gate](../sims/prime79_unknown_checksum_gate.cjs) shows **rank80 over all 80 affine variables** at all 79 79-rune packet phases for both canonical GP glyph and GP-prime register, as well as cyclic/sorted predecessor-exclusion ranks after dropping repeated-output packets. All 58 source-red p40/p15/p53 first-occurrence cyclic alphabet orders also have rank80 on the 95 source-clean packets at phase35. This exactly excludes any **stationary 79-column affine parity code** in those declared registers, including unknown fixed affine column pads/scales, without English or source-key scoring; a planted control recovered all 80 checksum parameters up to scale and 7,505 intact output ranks. Nonlinear transforms, changing coefficients, alternate clocks and hidden intermediate data remain open. The exceptional alignment identifies a narrowly defined *testable hypothesis*, not an authentic cipher key, recovered text, or proof of Cicada intent. [Exact original-source constraints, survivor and inverse](../im-results/prime79-radix29-packet-gate.md) · [Capacity reproducer](../sims/prime79_radix29_28_exact_gate.cjs) · [Exact source-language bound](../sims/prime79_first_rune_cpsat.py) · [Terminal-rune rank proof and blind recovery](../sims/prime79_terminal_linear_gate.cjs).

**Retrospectively selected cyclic 164×79 / 78-phase direct-GP register (conditional exclusion).** Under the postselected phase35 circular frame, each 79-rune packet has one salt transition and 78 payload differences, yielding **12,706** nonzero GP28 ranks and **86** zero-step sites. Any arbitrary fixed secret GP28 permutation per public phase preserves same-phase equality. Across **16** clock/salt-mix models, all-secret top-12 capacity is at most **7,549**, while every one of **204,462** contiguous Shakespeare windows has at least **9,608** top-12 symbols; the full top-k deficit forces **≥2,071** changes for every model/window. Allowing **312** arbitrary phase×four-group tables across **32** models still forces **≥335** changes for every Shakespeare window (maximum top-12 capacity **9,567**); Latin Vulgate's minimum top-12 count is **11,028**. Source-shaped 78- and 312-table plants recover all **12,706** ranks and preserve all 86 marker sites. This is a conditional direct-GP exclusion only; it does not authenticate the 35/79/78 framing or exclude within-phase evolving state, more than four group epochs, salt×column/per-packet rekeys, other registers or languages. No original plaintext/key. [Exact all-secret report](../im-results/field79-phase-allkey-direct-gp-capacity.md) · [78-phase reproducer](../sims/field79_phase_allkey_gate.cjs) · [four-group reproducer](../sims/field79_fourgroup_allkey_gate.cjs).

The nine injective **literal GP-prime residue alphabets** admit only the identity among projective field permutations. A stronger, embedding-independent theorem shows that **any fixed 29-symbol alphabet inside F79** admits at most **two direct affine keys** (identity and one reflection), regardless of its secret rune-to-field mapping. Even with freely changing keys, each original plaintext rune could occupy at most the two most frequent LP2 ciphertext glyphs, **492+482=974/12,956 (7.52%)**, incompatible with the independently measured **1,574.5 ± 33.3** GP-E occurrences in source-native solved-Cicada controls. This excludes that exact direct field-affine model under native plaintext assumptions; intermediate codecs or changing embeddings remain possible. The alternative **F59 quadratic-residue alphabet** has 58 projective symmetries, whose orders cannot directly realize the selected 28-cycle lag-six echo. [Proof and positive control](../im-results/red-f79-primitive-root-heldout.md) · [Original-source frequency capacity](../im-results/previous-cipher-sbox-invariant.md).

**Zero-step EA remains a *conditional reversible keyless anchor*.** The original 86 immediate ciphertext repeats have word roles **23 first, 19 last-not-first, 11 second-not-last, 33 other**. Matching original GP word boundaries to plaintext boundaries conflicts sharply with native solved-Cicada EA (zero word-final EA in the finite source) under a 2,000-draw reference. **Independent plaintext/ciphertext word boundaries change that conclusion:** the original four-bin role profile has conditional χ² **2.165**; **7,017/12,956** exact cyclic shifts of the same 86 marker sites across the unchanged ciphertext-word layout are as deviant, and **1,566/3,000** independently rotated native source plants score as high. From all 29 GP symbols, **EA alone** has a count-only native-reference residual below three source sampling SDs (expected **82.83±8.87**, observed 86; next NG has residual −4.68 SD). The red-source F29 generator **15** supports an exactly reversible 28-step chain with GP EA mapped to an unchanged ciphertext glyph. Its layout-matched synthetic controls invert **38,868,000/38,868,000** runes. However, its independently uniform pad misses all three original selected lag-5/6 echo counts in 1,000 source-layout plants, so the model is **incomplete and no original plaintext is claimed**. Keyless EA positions remain *hypothetical*. Two key-independent post-EA phase screens find no native-GP equality leakage from a shared restartable pad, even if each marker adds its own secret offset (original differential **2,689/75,390 = 0.035668** versus **0/120** genuine source controls as low). Two simple position/state clocks have now also failed under the conditional EA/log-15 model: the progressive counter K_i=(phase+i) mod 28 (448 settings; best score **−7.334815**) and the cumulative plaintext-sum accumulator K_(i+1)=K_i+P_i mod 28 (224 settings; best score **−7.337707**). Both outputs remain far below held-out Shakespeare GP (**−1.923133**) and have no coherent plaintext; their layout-matched controls invert exactly. This excludes these simple linear clocks, not all evolving state. The next priority is a distinct nonlinear/nonperiodic source-fixed state or independently derived interval key, followed by exact held-out original re-encryption. [Reconciled red-triangle study](../im-results/red-span-triangle.md) · [progressive-clock report](../im-results/red-triangle-progressive-ea-clock.md) · [cumulative-sum report](../im-results/ea-cumulative-plaintext-sum.md) · [cumulative-sum reproducer](../sims/ea_cumulative_plaintext_sum.cjs) · [progressive-clock reproducer](../sims/red_triangle_progressive_ea_clock.cjs) · [base reversible model](../sims/ea_zero_step_reversible.cjs) · [passed CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/42792523462).

**All-secret fixed-marker plaintext-state gate.** The original **85** nonzero steps following adjacent repeated ciphertext runes contain **all 28** nonzero GP29 values (largest multiplicity **8**). If a zero step exposes **one fixed unknown plaintext GP marker**, its following nonzero step must use one stationary secret output permutation indexed by that preceding plaintext marker. Any arbitrary secret bijection merely relabels the 85 observed values, requiring **all 28 other GP plaintext runes** to occur directly after the same marker. Exhaustively testing **all 29 possible markers against the full GP-encoded Shakespeare, Gibran and Emerson corpora**, the maximum number of distinct nonmarker successors anywhere in those entire texts is only **26/25/27** respectively. Across **581,244** 85-event windows from all marker classes, the maximum diversity is **24/23/23**. Two EA-marker and one E-marker full-length 29-secret-row source-native cipher plants each invert and reencrypt all **12,956** GP symbols exactly. Thus no stationary previous-plaintext-symbol successor-row cipher with one fixed zero marker can decrypt verbatim material from these three finite GP-English sources, regardless of the marker or the secret permutations. Evolving cipher state, other source registers and actual zero-marker meaning remain unresolved. [All-secret invariant, limits and controls](../im-results/ea-successor-allsecret-plainstate-gate.md) · [all-29 reproducible source gate](../sims/all29_marker_successor_support.cjs).

**LP2 pages 28/42.** The aligned residue-difference histogram is unusual among the 1,485 page pairs. Both unspaced exact bigram and trigram marginal likelihoods rank the source alignment first among 272 shifts under a previously selected scale-four shared-pad hypothesis. However, the **source's actual symbol ordering scores worse than 377 of 400 permutations of its exact same difference histogram** under the same unspaced trigram forward model; a genuine independently generated shared-key English-GP plant beats all 400 order shuffles. Thus the rank-one alignment's apparent support is a **selected marginal-frequency effect**, without independent GP-English sequential evidence. Earlier printed-word-boundary and printed-line-coordinate models also fail to identify a common pad. No unknown LP2 plaintext or authentic key has been recovered. [Reconciled exact inversion, histogram-conditioned null and plant](../im-results/page28-page42-relative-key-anomaly.md) · [successful source run](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/42848759211).

**Page-local 23/46 word-length harmonic.** This is exploratory, near the corrected selection threshold. The simple 23-column transposition failed. [Corrected analysis](../im-results/word_length_order_20261110.md).

**Nine 27×27 source-square all-secret bound.** The source's first **6,561=9×729** unsolved runes, preceded by the complete **729=27²** first three physical pages, form a conditional nine-tile source geometry. A direct GP29 family with **729 completely arbitrary shared-per-coordinate secret 29-rune permutations**, plus **any eight-way dihedral orientation and any fixed GP29 output Caesar offset on each tile**, preserves plaintext-rune equalities across panel pairs after undoing the orientation/offset. An exact affine-plane decomposition of all **36 tile pairs** into **12 nonoverlapping triangles** caps their total original-input plaintext equality count at **1,362/26,244**, even with all 729 secret alphabets and all tile keys unknown. All **3,000 nonoverlapping-window nine-panel GP-English controls** from Shakespeare, Gibran and Emerson are higher (minima **1,402/1,452/1,517**), and an independently generated secret-key positive exactly recovers/reencrypts **6,561/6,561** runes. This is a strong **conditional direct-language exclusion** for that specified shared-cell architecture, not evidence that the retrospective nine-square framing is the intended cipher or that any authentic key was found. The broader **per-tile output-affine** extension remains open across three native-GP reference sources: its original 12-triangle upper bound **1,524** is sharpened by an exact **84-triangle rational edge-cover certificate** to **≤1,511** of 26,244 equality pairs under all secret affine/D4 keys and 729 arbitrary shared coordinate S-boxes. All 1,000 Emerson controls (minimum 1,517) now fail this model; Shakespeare/Gibran reference minima 1,402/1,452 remain below its upper bound. An independently hidden affine-key native-GP plant in the prior SFT reconstructs 6,561/6,561 runes. [Exact theorem, source controls and scope](../im-results/lp2-nine-27tile-allsecret-capacity.md) · [Reproducer](../sims/lp2_ninetile_d4_caesar_sbox_gate.cjs) · [passing manual CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/42864784240).

**Fibonacci packet byte-register gate.** Under the provisional page-IV and transparent-doublet compacting, 12,815 nonrepeating GP28 steps form exactly 55 × 233 symbols. A strict 233-digit radix-28 to 140-byte direct ASCII transport allows at most **13** distinct packet-leading symbols in any globally fixed unknown alphabet. The original stream has **at least 20** distinct leaders for every one of **932** phase/direction/rank-convention cases, so the entire fixed-alphabet seven-bit ASCII subfamily is ruled out. Two source-native 7,700-byte plants invert with all 86 original doublet sites preserved; no original plaintext is recovered. [Fibonacci 55×233 all-key step-array and ASCII packet gates](../im-results/gp28-fibonacci-55x233-invariant.md).

**Fibonacci 55×233 matrix-transposition GP key limit.** Read the 12,815 nonrepeated GP28 predecessor ranks as a **55×233 rectangle**, in either cyclic/sorted rank convention and any row/column-transposed, independently reflected, serpentine traversal (**32 layouts**). Even with an *entirely arbitrary secret 28-way alphabet per phase* and all **141 page-IV/repeat markers unrestricted**, the exact ranked top-12 frequency limit excludes **all key periods 1–295** across all tested native-GP Shakespeare, Gibran and Emerson windows: **9,440 complete original-cipher all-key configurations**. Across periods up to 256 the maximal full-book upper bound is **9,328**, versus Shakespeare minimum **9,651** (at least **323** necessarily different runes). Period 296 is the first to escape this one frequency test, with no plaintext/key implied. The independent source-native plant recovers and reencrypts **12,956/12,956** runes. [Integrated original-source proof](../im-results/fibonacci233-nonzero-phase-capacity.md#matrix-transposed-55233-register-exact-all-secret-top-12-exclusion) · [Reproducer](../sims/fibonacci233_matrix_transpose_allkey.cjs) · [successful CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/42869008424).


**Nine-chapter stationary first-difference substitution (key-independent exclusion).** The original 12,901 within-page GP29 predecessor differences have nearly flat nonzero frequencies. Even allowing an independent, completely arbitrary 29! secret difference-to-plaintext alphabet in **each of the nine original red-heading chapters**, no chapter can match the symbol-frequency maximum of any of ten frozen native-GP Shakespeare/Gibran windows (90/90 comparisons). The closest per-chapter optimal reassignment still requires changing **3,638/12,901 (28.20%)** rune identities to match one control. Source-native secret-alphabet plants recover all **12,956** symbols exactly, including arbitrary page starts. This closes **stationary chapter-key differential substitution** as a direct English-GP inverse, while evolving state, variable-length and non-GP intermediates remain open. [Original-input bound and caveats](../im-results/lp2-delta-chapter-allsecret-capacity.md) · [standalone SFT](../sims/lp2_delta_chapter_allsecret_capacity_sft.cjs) · [successful manual Windows CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43247092196).

**Innermost SHED ring alignment (source-exact, decipherment still open).** Completed Issue #391 had already noted the **p55+p56+p57 = 256** total as an alternative to other four-ring hypotheses. A more discriminating original-text check now shows that the original pp49–51 **32×8** numerical table's four perimeter shell lengths **76/68/60/52** partition the final three LP2 pages (76 printed p55 runes (including one p54 word-carry), 85 solved p56, 95 clear p57) so the **innermost 52-cell ring begins exactly at p57 rune offset 43, the printed word SHED**, and contains the whole final imperative, “SHED OUR OWN CIRCUMFERENCES. FIND THE DIVINITY WITHIN AND EMERGE.” The 256 total is unique among 56 consecutive 3-page windows. The first interior shell break falls inside a p56 cipher word, and no source selects a cipher operator: creator intent remains unproven. Crucially, the 180 solved GP runes (with p56's only literal-F at index56 validated against its full known plaintext) form a **known-plaintext gate** for any proposed 256-cell decoder. Eight fixed ring reads, even with three independent arbitrary residue-to-rune codebooks, miss at least **35/68, 30/60 and 25/52** known symbols on the respective inner rings. An all-secret ringwise substitution-plus-**arbitrary-position-transposition** test also cannot reproduce the 180 independently solved inner symbols: at least **13/68 + 8/60 + 8/52 = 29/180** mismatches survive even after independently choosing every ring's 29! alphabet bijection and any position permutation; this test uses the fully **decrypted** p56 text and has exact native-GP positive controls ([all-secret ring SFT](../sims/lp2_terminal256_ring_allsecret_hist_sft.cjs)). A full **256-tap GF29 circular convolution**, treating p55 as 76 unknown plaintext symbols, has rank76 and **176 contradictions** in each original direction; an exact source-key synthetic plant recovers **76/76**. A separate **all-secret 8×8 toroidal GF29 convolution plus intercept** on the source's 32×8 table has **rank65** but **108–115 incompatible known-inner equations** under all eight source-corner shell orders and both solved-plain or original-ink p56 controls; eight source-native plants recover all 65 weights and predict 76/76 outer cells ([original-source SFT](../sims/lp2_terminal256_8x8_allsecret_kernel_sft.cjs), [bounded gate](../im-results/lp2-terminal256-knowninterior-ring-gate.md#full-88-toroidal-linear-stencil-exact-65-parameter-known-interior-obstruction-agent11-13-november-2026)). The physical p55 first rune `NG` completes a p54 word, so the 256 terminal glyphs divide into **255 page-body runes plus one inherited carry**. A literal GF(256) 255-nonzero-state cycle plus one sentinel is **incompatible** with the published table: its 256 values contain only **161 distinct bytes**, including **two zero bytes**. A separate frozen **GF(29²) [256,180] evaluation-code interpretation**, using the independently grid-derived primitive 19+13u and the exact 180 solved p56/p57 symbols to polynomial-interpolate 76 outer predictions, also fails: only **2/76** outputs lie in the GP29 subfield and **0/76** equal the actual p55 runes; fixed basis/trace/norm projections match only [6,5,4,0] of76. The full 76/76 planted ECC control passes [manual Node22 CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43270101674) ([gate](../im-results/lp2-terminal256-gf841-rs180-parity-gate.md), [SFT](../sims/lp2_terminal256_gf841_rs180_parity_sft.cjs)). This is a finite negative for the specified field-code model; alternative key/frame formats need a new source instruction. Under the eight original fixed ring orders, a fully secret stationary raw-byte→GP29 codebook has at least **46/180** contradictory known assignments, while an independent GP29 additive-inverse model with an arbitrary byte→mask map fails at least **30/180**. The newer **512-ring-orientation** all-secret extension is strictly stronger for stationary raw-byte→direct-GP29 lookup: even granting **256 independently unknown GP29 codebook entries**, every inner-ring layout requires at least **43/180** known-rune changes under the globally optimal secret codebook (the earlier 44 is a greedy first-assignment witness; the best arrangement sees 127 distinct source bytes). The table's natural printed row-major order independently requires **47/180** known-rune changes under any byte codebook; a separate 65-unknown-value additive high/low base-60-digit lookup also fails all **512** layouts, with **106** inconsistent dependent equations in the best deterministic elimination order. A stronger same-source test grants **a separate arbitrary 256-entry secret codebook to each of the three inner rings**, checks **every one** of each ring's cyclic starts and both directions, and still finds **zero exact fits** (minimum contradictions **5/68**, **5/60** and **4/52** across **136, 120 and 104** orientations). Thus the 68-rune p56-only ring and the clear 52-rune p57-only ring independently contradict any stationary byte→GP29 decoder, without requiring one shared p56/p57 key. Exact per-ring original-cell plants pass. No unknown plaintext follows. These are conditional original-input exclusions, leaving context-dependent and evolving-state mechanisms open ([extended all-secret test](../im-results/lp2-terminal256-base60-digit-lookup-gate.md), [SFT](../sims/lp2_terminal256_base60_digit_lookup_sft.cjs), [manual CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43347500530); [earlier source-corrected terminal decision](../im-results/lp2-terminal256-knowninterior-ring-gate.md#corrected-255-body-gf256-register-gate-original-180-rune-holdout-12-november-2026), [original SFT](../sims/lp2_terminal255_gf256_original_gate_sft.cjs)). A further **known-plaintext-only** all-secret GF29 gate assigns independent unknown outputs to every one of the **127 distinct 30×6 interior tokens** plus an arbitrary row and column offset for each original source coordinate; against the **180 authentic p56+p57 plaintext runes**, its **167-secret-variable** system has coefficient rank **161** and **19 nonzero residual constraints**, hence **zero possible exact decoders** even in this large separable lookup family. The original 180-cell positive plant fits exactly. No unknown p55 data is fitted; [source-original solver and precise scope](../im-results/lp2-p50-known180-token-rowcol-allsecret-gate.md); [successful native original-input CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43384244825). The exact byte fingerprint #708 finds 161 distinct bytes (95 repeated extras), including equal values at positions 7 and 14, and no direct match to AES/MD2/RC4/Twofish S-boxes, SHA-256 K, MD5 T, or three archived 2014 onion captures. This excludes every direct 256-entry byte permutation and the tested exact-copy tables; no published source or consumer is identified. Any further table use requires a separately selected non-permutation or stateful operation. A further complete 2D original-input test permits **512 independent inner-ring orders** and **all 29 GF29 byte slopes**: every 30×6 residual has full rank **6**, excluding any sum of up to five stationary row-column products; the best additive row+column trial satisfies only **282/6,525** necessary minors. All secret stationary 3×3+intercept and 5×5+intercept original-grid linear filters are likewise incompatible with the **180/112** known inner targets across all 512 orders, while matching plants are recovered exactly. A final all-secret additive GF29 mask test finds that the 180 known interior symbols have **minimum linear-recurrence order 89** across the same 512 ring orientations (497 require order90, 14 order91). The source-linked 2013 BASE29/p39 product polynomial of order4 fails at least **160/176** masked recurrence equations, and a planted source-factor positive control passes. [Combined 2D and recurrence result](../im-results/lp2-terminal256-original-inner-separable-gates.md) · [successful CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43260125837). Thus these additional direct models are excluded, with no recovered p55 plaintext ([original-inner separability study](../im-results/lp2-terminal256-original-inner-separable-gates.md); [CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43256330152)). [Original clue, controls and caveats](../im-results/lp2-terminal256-knowninterior-ring-gate.md) · [SFT](../sims/lp2_terminal256_knowninterior_ring_sft.cjs) · [Windows CI pass](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43253477349).

**Repeated terminal pair (intermediate clue, not plaintext).** DJU BEI is the only adjacent two-word type repeated in the unknown corpus, at p27 offset 28 and p55 offset 70; the two copies are identical. A source-derived coordinate relation is exact: solved p57 word WE has GP-prime sum 86; p27's red title pair P/P also sums 86; page step +86 mod58 maps p57→p27, and φ(86)=42 maps p27 offset 28→p55 offset 70. WE is the only one of 20 solved p57 words whose joint page/offset update reaches the second bookend. This is a navigation/bookend observation, not an authenticated cipher: the 34 aligned positions cannot be explained by a stationary GP29 substitution (six terminal identities force six mappings, while the 28 preceding positions contain nine conflicting input→output assignments), and no source selects a changing-state update. #438 closed without a source-selected inverse or plaintext.


**p56 factorization / p55 UDP retrieval candidate (12 November 2026).** p56 explicitly asks for a deep-web page that hashes to its 64-byte target; it does not instruct factoring. An analyst factorization gives `N = 84 × 281 × 2,155,279,523 × Q`, with Q a 465-bit strong probable prime. Big-endian 2,155,279,523 maps to **128.118.244.163**, 281 is IANA **personal-link**, and 84 matches a 76-byte p55 page-local BASE29 body plus an 8-byte UDP header. The signed 2013 BASE29 example supplies alphabet 0–9A–S. Q is 59 bytes, but the 2014 unsigned `59.txt` reference names a **page number**, not a measured file length; the former “59-byte match” has no force. The factor 281 is the 60th prime (zero-based ordinal 59), another possible but post-selected link to that filename. The p55 BASE29 ASCII payload begins `LP` (the book initials) exactly at the page boundary, unique among 55 rune-bearing unknown pages, though 22 further `LP` pairs appear internally. A full original-page look-elsewhere check finds that **281 also equals header8 + 273 runes on six other pages (pp11,17,21,25,41,44)**, so the p56 factorization cannot independently choose 281 as a UDP *port* instead of a packet *length*. A source-port-zero/checksum-zero RFC 768 specimen has header `0000011900540000`, but no source-selected reply port or server protocol. The p3/p39 SKY/UDP reading and 2013 network clue provide context, but this is an unverified retrieval/key hypothesis, not an authenticated endpoint or recovered preimage. p55's first rune is an inherited NG carry, so body-only length is 75; the 84-byte match uses the full page-local sequence or a framing byte. No packet was sent and no host was probed. See [full factorization / UDP report](../im-results/p56-factorization-udp-endpoint-hypothesis.md) and [reproducer](../sims/p56_factorization_udp_endpoint_sft.cjs).

## 4. Well-tested *bounded* negative families

**Grid-nonic all-secret plaintext-singleton exclusion (13 November 2026).** The source pp49–51 conventional-base60 GF29 polynomial's exact degree-nine factor defines one candidate nine-state additive keystream. Keeping original printed word boundaries and ordinary GP-native English one-letter words A/I/O, nine independent original singleton positions determine the entire nine-symbol secret initial state from just **3⁹ = 19,683** possibilities, without enumerating all **29⁹ = 14.5 trillion** key states. On the untouched pp0–55 text, **zero** continuous-key states satisfy all **83** original one-rune words (best **29/83**). The independently source-visible **pp15–22** red-rubric chapter likewise rejects every fresh local nine-state key on its **18** singleton words (best **14/18**), refuting the complete nine-visual-chapter-reset variant under the identical model. Fully length- and singleton-matched native-GP planted keys are uniquely recovered at **83/83** and **18/18**; both SFTs passed in [original-source CI](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43391255611). This excludes only a GP29 additive nonic LFSR with preserved word boundaries and the stated continuous/chapter clocks; secret output alphabets, nonlinear encoders, other resets and non-English plaintext remain open. No previously unknown plaintext or authentic key was recovered. [Consolidated report](../im-results/lp2-nonic-singleword-allsecret-gate.md) · [Complete all-secret SFT](../sims/lp2_nonic_singleword_allseed_gf29_sft.cjs).

**Opening p0 stave / branch-rune candidate (bounded negative).** The 3|3 side mark and first black rune TH admit one local Younger Futhark 6:5:5 reading, but no exact pre-2014 source or second independent prediction; the same image rule gives A on p1 and OE on p2. This is not an alternative 29-symbol alphabet or key. The p0 infinity-like motif has no source-selected seam/handedness (#437); the p0 red 13-rune preamble has no exact source match in the finite 2012–2013 panel; direct GP transliteration (NG/ING variants) and the 8:5 monoalphabetic pattern with the first-black-TH holdout produced zero candidates (#446 closed; [report](../im-results/p0-red-header-historical-source-gate.md), [reproducer](../sims/p0_red_header_source_gate.cjs)).

**Derived p32 Micro QR M1 all-secret finder exclusion (bounded).** The 121 source-transcribed p32 runes, reshaped into an **unprinted** 11×11 GP matrix, cannot represent a literal Micro QR M1 through any fixed secret rune-to-bit map. All **25** geometrically possible 7×7 finder placements remain contradictory even after granting independent unknown XOR masks for each row and each column. A **four-source-cell parity certificate** makes the upper-left standard finder impossible regardless of the secret mapping; real synthetic hidden-color finders pass. This excludes one direct binary visual decoder, leaving independently specified transpositions and nonlinear maps open. [Source-exact SFT](../sims/lp2_p32_microqr_allsecret_finder_sft.cjs) · [Source/evidence note](../im-results/lp2-p32-microqr-finder-allsecret-gate.md).

**Original p32 signed-image-axis 2D prefix-sum inverse (bounded negative).** The 2016 image dimensions 563×569 exactly match the unique first-row/first-column GP-prime sums of original p32, but all six frozen horizontal, vertical and mixed finite-difference inverses of the 10×10 interior remain nonlinguistic. The best candidate is not distinguished by the familywise max-of-six calibration: **627/1,000** matched English windows equal or exceed it (plus-one p≈**0.62737**). This rejects only the six specified prefix-sum variants; the source-axis correspondence remains an observation, not a selected cipher operation. [Report](../im-results/p32-source-axis-2d-prefix-inverse.md) · [SFT](../sims/p32_source_axis_2d_prefix_inverse_sft.cjs).

**Terminal p53 + enlarged p54 A + p55 256-position frame (bounded direct-codebook exclusions).** The source-visible 179+1+76 composite fills 256 positions. The complementary literal grid geometry places the 76-cell p49–51 border against p55 and its 180-cell interior against p53's 179 runes plus the first enlarged p54 A; allowing two completely arbitrary stationary secret input-token maps and every 76-start/two-direction perimeter orientation still forces **at least 52/256** original-symbol disagreements (best 204/256). This excludes that precise complete regionwise lookup, without choosing the cross-chapter receiver or decoding a glyph ([source-bound SFT](../sims/lp2_p50_dualregion_stationary_allsecret_sft.cjs)). Both standard 8-column transposition directions fail (best score −11.80083 versus raw −11.78729; all 843 disjoint Emerson controls outrank the best candidate). A complementary full-frame value-lookup check needs at least **90/256** substitutions for byte→rune and **222/256** for rune→byte; the existing 255/256 omitted-cell gate remains the broader byte→rune exclusion, while the reverse mapping result is new. Both arbitrary-map positive controls recover exactly. A further source-order bytewise-pad test uses the p50 bytes against the 256-rune frame with GP indices or GP prime weights as keys: XOR and both additive directions (eight fixed models) all remain random-like (printable ratio 32.8–40.6%, longest run at most five bytes, entropy 7.09–7.25 bits/byte), while all eight native-text plants recover exactly. This retires only these direct bytewise pads; the framing remains structural, not a selected cipher. These negatives exclude only the named fixed direct transformations, not the retrospective framing or keyed/stateful/nonlinear operations ([consolidated report](../im-results/tree-outline-blur-crosspage.md#full-256-position-value-based-lookup-gate-agent-36-11-november); [columnar SFT](../sims/terminal_p54_eight_column_transposition_sft.cjs); [lookup SFT](../sims/grid256_terminal_composite_lookup_gate.cjs)).
**p54 Ac24+232 / 256-grid-tail alignment (bounded negative).** The oversized red A has GP index24 and the original p54 page has232 runes, leaving a 29×8 table tail after a 24-cell skip. Direct additive variants and a further 36-model heading-keyed columnar/additive family remain ciphertext-like: best GP trigram −11.58322 versus worst 232-symbol native-GP Gibran holdout −8.65111; a source-native plant recovers exactly. This rejects only the frozen p54-tail operations, not the numerical framing or other stateful transforms ([report](../im-results/p54-ac24-232-plus-24-grid256-source-gate.md#eight-rune-heading-as-a-column-key-bounded-negative); [SFT](../sims/p54_ac24_heading_columnar_tail_sft.cjs); [original additive SFT](../sims/p54_ac24_grid256_tail_sft.cjs)).

**Facing-tree pp10/11 → p55 direct sevenfold stationary-codebook gate (bounded).** The original page lengths **263+273=536=7×76+4**, and marked GP index 24 has order seven. Across five four-rune p10 edge splits, two page orders, two sevenfold layouts and three source-motivated weights (**60** frozen variants), even an arbitrary non-injective stationary (F_{29}\to F_{29}) output map can match at most **34/76** p55 runes; at least 42 must disagree. The solved-p57 native plant recovers 76/76. This retires only the direct sevenfold/stationary-codebook reading, not nonlinear or stateful transformations ([report](../im-results/tree-facing-pages-sevenfold-p55.md); [SFT](../sims/tree_facing_pair_p55_sevenfold_gate.cjs)).

These eliminate specified mechanisms under their documented source and repair assumptions. They do not prove that LP2 is unbreakable or that every stateful, variable-length or alternate-language cipher is excluded.

The historical 2014 stage11 `sexagesimal.bin` was **copied and packed from three earlier human-readable page transcriptions**, 49/50/51, whose 256 original upper base-60 tokens reproduce **every byte**. The original 2014 50.txt lower numeric block **already explicitly corrected** positions 165 (47→18), 170 (194→254) and 172 (174→148), although the later archived binary retained the old token values; the seven residual original-print differences are exclusively I/i or L/l case readings ([2014 source chain](../im-results/cicada2014-sexagesimal-upstream-transcription.md), [SFT](../sims/cicada2014_sexagesimal_upstream_sft.cjs)). The original Stage11 signed instructions also correct the upload direction: `/cgi-bin/upload` is hosted by the participant; Cicada's client GETs the participant's `/key.asc` and POSTs a clear-signed message. Cicada's own `/cgi-bin/squares` is a separate form endpoint. A contemporaneous participant archive, `micheloosterhof/cicada-2014`, added the 73-line Onion6 form (`stage10/index.html`, blob `9d7c35bc8fff24c8079668c9d070d98277502f7c`) and static confirmation page (`stage10/squares`, blob `d71303af76016c8e88ed5dcc8a18b08e6276a437`) in a **2014-02-13** commit; the form was only renamed in a 2014-06-02 commit with the same blob. The form is plain HTML: POST `/cgi-bin/squares`, fields `53301`, `71033`, `51033`, `onion` in document order, default `application/x-www-form-urlencoded`, no JavaScript/CSS, hidden inputs, or custom client transform. The three square labels mean 5×5 sum3301, 7×7 sum1033 and 5×5 sum1033. #477 CLOSED: no original Cicada-side validator, accepted POST body or numerical acceptance rules survive beyond those labels; the form is not a server response. The exact historical POST body and participant onion value are not preserved. The 152-byte confirmation page is a static thank-you with three linked original JPEG assets, not the handler. The contemporary archive retains each image: `/107.jpg`, `/167.jpg`, `/229.jpg` are **byte-identical to first-release Liber Primus LP1 pages 14, 15, 16 in order**, with exact matching Git blob SHA-1s `28a1ac59a2f1f51d272e4e362c0c35b4be241347`, `32952b7d28028f5fedffceace4de0e7eb688d3d6`, `6f8059f7e8025bf3f51fceefe20be0cce8ce1e20` ([source artifact handoff and offline SFT](../im-results/2014-stage10-three-images-lp1-source-handoff.md)). Their numeric filenames have prime ranks 28/39/50, but no historical instruction selects those as **LP2** page pointers; the exact image contents establish a direct Stage10-to-LP1 handoff. The 2024 iBotPeaches and 2026 krisyotam copies are later mirrors. #514 CLOSED: the original LP1 p05 Wisdom 5×5 matrix exactly equals the third Stage10 5×5/1033 matrix in resources/cicada2014-openpuff-magic-squares.txt; this is a source-authenticated valid candidate input, not proof of unique acceptance or an LP2 key. #522 CLOSED: the p33 1033/3301 header does not reference the Stage10 form; no p33/LP2 pointer, checksum or shared instruction survives. #489 CLOSED: fixed top-left pp49–51 page blocks fail. #527 CLOSED: the exact Stage10 matrices cannot be literal submatrices of the 256-cell LP2 table: table values are 0–255, while 23/25, 11/49 and 8/25 source-matrix entries exceed 255; all 276 intact 2-D and 672 contiguous flat placements yield 0/948 exact embeddings. No source selects an encoding transform. #494 CLOSED: no original Cicada-side `/cgi-bin/squares` validator or byte-preserved HTTP transaction (actual POST body plus status/headers and response) survives. The contemporaneous 2014 participant archive does preserve the exact 152-byte successful response body `stage10/squares` (Git blob SHA-1 `d71303af76016c8e88ed5dcc8a18b08e6276a437`; SHA-256 `94e705cd5ab79e1c6b6dcfb98cf0b00aaebb48fc0dfecf2c3f93b31f9bf615aa`): a generic thank-you followed only by `/107.jpg`, `/167.jpg`, `/229.jpg`. A participant wiki record and a later first-person account corroborate that this followed a completed square submission; neither preserves the actual POST body or timestamp. The response contains no square-use instruction, and the linked images are byte-identical to LP1 pages 14–16. The preserved `tools/check-onions.sh` writes `wget -S` output to `stage10/tmp/log`, but its command is a recursive GET mirror, not a POST to `/cgi-bin/squares`; the log path has no Git history and is absent from the 2014 tree. Even a recovered copy would not be the successful submission transaction. The archived `cgi.py` is a never-used participant upload mock, not the squares handler. Fetching the public key does not prove how Cicada used it ([squares-handler and client-source boundary](../im-results/2014-stage10-squares-cgi-source-boundary.md)) ([direction correction](../im-results/2014-stage11-upload-direction-correction.md); [form source gate](../im-results/cicada2014-onion6-form-response-source-gate.md)). The proposed eight-bit `01100110` (`f`) is not an independent channel: one sign bit is the already annotated correction at index 172, and the remaining seven are only I/i or L/l transcription ambiguities. The retrospective GF(29) polynomial-variant fit is within-source compatibility, not an author-selected checksum or key: its coefficient serialization and parity constraints were chosen with the complete LP2 table in view, and GF(29) cannot distinguish the index-165 alternatives 47 and 18 (difference 29). See the [explicit parity-screen limitation](../im-results/lp2-grid-11-original-glyph-adjudication.md#retrospective-gf29-consistency-gate-for-ten-historical-witness-disagreements). This *solver-derived* byte stream still differs from the canonical original LP2 printed table at ten positions, and the stale binary fails 12 direct raw-file digest tests plus 36 terminal-ring compositions ([source manifest](../resources/cicada2014-sexagesimal-source.json); [updated grid/hash gate](../im-results/terminal-grid-xor-anend-digest.md)). The table's 76-cell clockwise perimeter was also tested as a one-to-one GP29 key for p55 under eight fixed variants; every output scored below all 22,621 held-out native 76-rune Gibran windows ([perimeter-key gate](../im-results/terminal-grid-xor-anend-digest.md)). A separate 9-state quotient-perimeter Gronsfeld test uses the frozen 76-cell outer ring and `q=floor(B/29)`; all four base60/base59, ± variants also score below every held-out native window (best −12.01238 vs worst native −9.74839). **The original p49 marker has 10 red dots, whereas p55 has 13; their former alleged shared 3×3 terminator is a documented source error.** Thus the 76/76 match selects no such additive key ([updated test](../im-results/terminal-grid-xor-anend-digest.md)). Earlier experiments using those five altered cells remain valid counterfactual-source tests; they do not establish alternative original bytes. The historically proposed **base-59** reading changes **220/256** byte values relative to base-60. The exact pre-2014 base-60 alphabet is documented by the February 2007 MIRACL manual (`0–9,A–Z,a–x`) and independently by Sendmail queue-ID conventions; this makes base60 the stronger conventional working interpretation, but no Cicada artifact links p50 to either implementation, so authorial radix remains conditional ([source note](../resources/external/miracl-2007-base60-alphabet.md); [radix study](../im-results/cicada2014-sexagesimal-upstream-transcription.md#source-level-radix-gate-base-60-versus-f-omitting-base-59)). The authentic solved-p56 64-byte hash, treated directly as SHAKE256/HMAC-SHA512/SHA512-counter GP28/GP29 streams over the complete 12,956-rune original text, yielded a **frozen heldout English score −6.187430**, no improvement over raw source ciphertext **−6.186941**, versus independent true-key source heldout **−2.374419**, despite **12,956/12,956** source-key plant recovery. A distinct full 512-bit-digest-as-CPython2.7-MT19937-long-seed test across **96** GP29/GP28, two-endian, three-clock, four-projection and sign variants also fails on the unchanged original: best preselected training mode scores **−7.43007** in training and **−7.52781** on withheld pp28–55, versus raw full-book **−7.44015** (English quadgrams). All 12,956 GP symbols recover in its exact-key native synthetic plant, with the wrong digest endian rejected; no plaintext or historically specified Python seed use ([new original-input SFT](../sims/anend_hash_python27_longseed_original_sft.cjs); [passing manual Node.js 22 original-input CI run 43381316529](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43381316529)). Other digest-derived direct nonces also fail under source-grid AES/ChaCha. One additional source-fixed relation is negative: the canonical 256-byte pp49–51 base-60 payload is not the direct SHA-512 preimage of p56, nor is any of its four aligned 64-byte quarters under any of eight row/column and reversal readings ([report](../im-results/pp49-51-base60-anend-hash-sft.md); [reproducer](../sims/pp49_51_base60_anend_hash_sft.py)). This does not test keyed, salted, KDF, PRF or encrypted uses. A new four-serialization original AES/ChaCha inverse (all exact 8! column assignments, 16 modes) produces only **0.03480172** GP IoC on an unfitted 2,100-rune continuation; **26/120** jointly shuffled-source refits match or outperform it. A distinct pair-consuming rejection inverse tests the same 256-byte base60 table as a periodic GP29 key under the exact j+=2 transition (the first skipped candidate must collide with the previous ciphertext; the second draw is unconstrained). With the rejection rate fixed by the 86/12,901 source doublet count, its nonzero-rejection solved-LP1 plant recovers 179/179 runes, but the original 55 pages remain nonlinguistic under all four page-reset row-major/reverse and ± sign settings in both Shakespeare English (best global −5.422 nats/rune) and Latin Vulgate (−6.093). This closes only the direct base60-byte-mod29 key under the stated pair-skip family, not other source keys or clocks ([report](../im-results/grid-keyskip2-pair-transition.md); [reproducer](../sims/grid_keyskip2_viterbi.cjs)). A corrected base-59 red-title KDF scan over **96 schemes** fails in both English and Latin GP; its fitted English key also yields only **−5.80612 nats/rune** on a fresh 1,120-rune third block (**9/64** matched source shuffles outperform or tie), while a true source-key GP plant recovers **1,120/1,120** symbols. A source-locked **32-row/32-fragment RC4** inverse (one 8-byte grid row per 13-dot-delimited fragment; base59/base60, drop0/256, low/high projection and both signs) also fails: best first-45%-per-fragment IoC **0.03458607**, heldout **0.03448484**, against uniform **0.03448276**. Its blinded base59/drop256/high/−1 plant ranks first and recovers **12,956/12,956**. A distinct 576-model 13/31 LCG row/column permutation of the canonical 32×8 grid, treating each eight-byte row as a per-fragment DES key, also fails: best training score **−6.05751** falls to held-out **−6.22898**, worse than raw **−6.18534**; its native 12,956-rune plant recovers exactly and ranks the true configuration first ([reproducer](../sims/fragment_grid_des_lcg_inverse.cjs); [CI 42943557918](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/42943557918)). Unknown-alphabet hidden-skip source inversions likewise fail. An independent source-red p53 **51-rune** test cannot encode three known 16-character historical onion labels under any fixed 29-symbol permutation (**26/51** maximum exact digits), or the solved-p56 additive prime-clock source model within **2 million** clock offsets (**6/17** best prefix); blinded synthetic cribs work. The same red51 and preceding black128 byte/nonce constructions authenticated **zero of 39,744** complete AES-256-GCM/ChaCha20-Poly1305 240-byte grid payloads with 16-byte tags under base59/base60 table readings, while both planted AEAD originals authenticated exactly. A separate source-selected per-page GP29 pad gate tested the three 256-byte onion candidates against all 55 rune-bearing pages (41,580 byte/nibble/pair/reversal, offset-0–20, both-sign configurations). The p57 synthetic control recovered 95/95 runes, but the best real score (−6.39684) remained below the conservative family-wise 1% bar (−5.74352); no direct per-page additive pad survives. This excludes only these tested direct pad interpretations, not every possible use of the historical objects ([report](../im-results/onion-256-per-page-mod29-pad-gate.md); [reproducer](../sims/lp2_onion_256_per_page_mod29_pad_gate.cjs)).

A new 240-key family from 15 XOR subsets of three historical 256-byte hidden-service excerpts plus the corrected grid gives **0.034318** on an untouched 2,100-rune third block versus 100 source-shuffle controls mean **0.034510** (**72/100** outrank); none yields plaintext. Four independently red-ink-derived 29-rune cyclic orders all violate the exact phase35 79/78 numeric capacity bound. Existing centered-rank proofs also exclude fixed-coordinate eight-column grid polynomial shares of degree≤6 in three tested fields. A separate direct cross-source test of **all 6,080** natural Shamir degree≤2 share settings across three archived 256-byte hidden-service response snapshots (including mutable Stage03 onion2) plus the 256-byte LP2 grid found **0/6,080 full fits**, with at most six of 256 byte equalities; A coordinate-free GF(2) rank proof additionally excludes **all nonconstant directly seven-bit ASCII outputs** from four arbitrary fixed GF256-linear share coefficients plus an unknown constant XOR byte, in both tested fields and four byte layouts under either grid version, while leaving binary secrets open. An exact companion reconstruction also inverted unknown GF256 coefficients from **15 binary-file signatures at every valid offset** across two families and the same source layouts: **0/119,456** original header fits, with four fully recovered 256-byte planted files; unrestricted binary secrets still remain open.  4-of-4 cubic-derived AES/ChaCha keys likewise fell to **0.034381** third-block IoC, indistinguishable from 25 source-shuffled refits (**11/25** outrank), while a true eight-key GP-English plant was recovered. OutGuess headers alone give false positives. These are bounded direct families, not exclusions of other KDFs, changing keys or encrypted intermediates A separate 3×3-dot-motivated quotient-channel probe uses `B=29q+r`: direct `r±q` on the 256 base60 bytes and `q` as a continuous Gronsfeld key for p49+p51 flanking text both remain nonlinguistic (best mean English-quadgram scores −7.2933 and −7.3272); this retires only these fixed additive uses, not the q channel itself ([report](../im-results/p50-base29-high-digit-radix9-source-gate.md); [SFT](../sims/p50_base29_high_digit_radix9_sft.cjs)). A separate direct recurrence gate tests the 256 historical/canonical table residues in row/column order against H, G and H·G in both coefficient directions: only 4–14 of 252/254 windows have zero residual and all 29 residues occur, so the table is not itself a direct H/G LFSR output ([reproducer](../sims/p50_hg_direct_recurrence_gate.cjs)). This does not exclude other feedback-state consumers. ### Punctuation identity stream (source-audited inventory; not a decryption)

The derivative transcription contains 180 events (145 ⁘, four ⁚, 31 ⁜). Four ⁘ events on physical p15 (JPEG 32.jpg, event indices 57–60 at page-local offset 9 / global rune offset 3,612) are editorial numeric-row terminators absent from the original image. The remaining 141 stops and four commas have been checked against the original JPEGs: **138 stops are four-dot diamonds** (137 black, one red), **one stop is a three-dot comma-form**, **one is a single-dot ambiguous placement**, and **one has no corresponding source glyph**; all four commas are three-dot zigzags. Four events are physically printed at the left edge of the following line, so the inventory preserves both transcription and physical line numbers. The 145-row audit finds 144 visible small marks and one absent stop. The separate 31 ⁜ large-mark records remain the existing 31/31 image-annotation cross-match.

The 180-event transcription inventory decomposes into **175 visible source marks** (31 large + 138 diamonds + five three-dot forms + one ambiguous singleton) and **five nonphysical events** (four editorial p15 row terminators plus the absent p37 stop). The 31 large marks comprise 28 thirteen-dot and three ten-dot records, but occupy only **29 distinct rune offsets**: same-offset double marks occur at **1860** (p7 black/red 13-dot pair) and **3603** (p15 adjacent 13-dot pair). Since the last mark is exactly at rune offset **12956**, a nominal 31-mark/32-fragment clock has **three zero-rune intervals**, leaving only **29 nonempty intervals**. A literal mapping of 32 nonempty cipher fragments onto the 32 printed table rows therefore fails without a source-specified rule for empty states ([bounded #484 source event-clock gate](../im-results/lp2-punctuation-identity-stream.md#large-mark-boundaries-and-32-fragment-reset-clock-limit-484-12-november-2026)). The physical forms are heterogeneous and do not establish a common state clock. The 30×6 table interior, 180 standard primes p21–p200 and 85+95 solved-page tail remain numerical equalities at the transcription level only; they do not select a reversible operation or recover plaintext.

**Finite historical clause-length crib gate (Issue #349).** Using 138 four-dot stops, five three-dot comma-form marks and 28 thirteen-dot unit records (26 unique rune offsets) gives 169 intervals over all 12,956 original LP2 runes. The fixed 13-source corpus contains 6,991 GP-normalized words and 904 source intervals. Its longest exact match is two intervals. The only type-exact pair is Book of the Law I:20–21, lengths 54 and 36 at LP2 offsets 8423–8513; its next two source intervals predict 25 and 23 at offsets 8538 and 8561, while LP2 has 431 and 189 ending at 8944 and 9133. The holdout fails. Two 2,000-trial within-source nulls give length-only add-one p=1.0; type-aware p=0.9260 and 0.9075. The second source clause also depends on treating & as zero letters and ignoring a colon. This retires only the finite preserved-clause-length crib hypothesis, not other punctuation mechanisms. Solved controls: LP1 page 1 45/45 words, p56 25/25, p57 20/20; 2014 LP1 page-05 Wisdom prose 3/4 clauses exact.

[Source-audited decision](../im-results/lp2-punctuation-identity-stream.md) · [145-event pixel inventory](../im-results/lp2-punctuation-source-audit.csv.gz) · [source-audit reproducer](../sims/lp2_punctuation_source_audit.py) · [clause-length reproducer](../sims/lp2_punctuation_clause_length_crib_sft.cjs) · [transcription event inventory and audit metadata](../resources/liber-primus/lp2-punctuation-identity.json).


**Static predecessor-ciphertext bijection entropy gate (12 November 2026).** For a book-wide fixed secret permutation of GP29 selected only by the previous ciphertext rune, exact finite-sample entropy preservation gives H(Cᵢ | Cᵢ₋₁) = H(Pᵢ | Cᵢ₋₁) ≤ H(Pᵢ). The original 12,901 within-page transitions require **at least 4.785306737 bits/rune** of marginal plaintext entropy, compared with **4.23997–4.27368** for canonical Shakespeare/Gibran/Emerson native-GP controls. A 12,956-rune fully reversible Shakespeare plant matches all original page sizes, the exact **86** adjacent equalities and almost exactly the original **4.8565-bit** marginal ciphertext entropy, yet its predecessor-conditional entropy is only **4.21298 bits**. This key-independent exclusion applies to the fixed 29-row previous-ciphertext permutation family, not changing secret states, page-dependent alphabets or non-GP plaintext, and recovers no unknown original text. [Exact proof and controls](../im-results/lp2-predecessor-entropy-bound.md) · [CommonJS SFT](../sims/lp2_predecessor_permutation_entropy_sft.cjs).

## 5. Research decisions with a path to actual decryption

1. **A source-constrained, reversible original-cipher inverse.** Freeze an independently justified key/state derivation from the original ink, page layout, 256-entry table or historical source material. Specify page/word resets and the treatment of all observed repeated outputs. A credible result must recover previously unfitted LP2 runes and **exactly re-encrypt** them. Use existing authentic solved-Cicada regressions and the [contextual conditional inverse](../sims/lag6_repair_contextual_inverse.cjs) as tools, with its architecture explicitly treated as provisional.

2. **Architecture discrimination before another large key sweep.** A proposed forward model should reproduce the *same heldout manuscript intervals* at the word-boundary, page-boundary, lag-five-cluster, lag-six, word-equality and printed-line levels. Report which features motivated its construction. A bounded original-JPEG test of inter-word dot geometry and vertical line placement across LP2 pp0–2, 16–21 and 40–48, with solved pp56–57 controls, found no independent spacing alphabet: 37 repeated exact rune-pair boundaries vary by at most 1 px within each pair, and 192/201 measured line intervals follow a 188–189 px grid; the remaining nine are enlarged paragraph intervals. Preserve the printed layout in any forward model, but do not treat these measurements as key material without a new independent selector ([report](../im-results/word-spacing-baseline-channel.md); [reproducer](../sims/word_spacing_baseline_channel.py)). The full-page follow-up measured the remaining 38 original pages (pp3–15, 22–39, 49–55) plus solved p56/p57: 389 accepted dark-text rows, 1,623 marked inter-rune gaps (all 9/10 px), and 319/340 anchored line intervals at 188/189 px; all remaining intervals and offsets are explained by visible paragraph/numbered-text breaks or rubric/art boundaries. No source-selected positional-to-plaintext rule was found (#474 CLOSED; [report](../im-results/lp2-remaining-source-micro-layout-gate.md)). Retire ordinary-text spacing-only bit/lane hypotheses; red ornaments and JPEG entropy remain separate. The current lossy repair surrogates can match some selected summaries without yielding a decryptable inverse. Require a recoverable hidden state or a mathematically lossless encoding.

   **F5 steganalysis is now at a valid positive-control stage, but not yet a valid original-image classifier.** Pinned F5 v1.0.0 coefficient-level embedding with ForceK=2/4 has extractor-verified 256/1024/4096-byte payloads on a genuine one-component grayscale LP2 p45 cover and an RGB LP2 p54 cover, with no quantized-DCT serialization differences. The official 2007 JET v2.2 274-D extractor is built. A 13-family pilot produced 0/10 held-out true positives and 0/10 false positives (training GroupKFold balanced accuracy 0.50), so it demonstrates inadequate sensitivity and **must not** support an absence claim. A preregistered 24-training/50-held-out-family validation (100 clean +100 planted controls) is running; original LP2 scoring is gated on at least 90% sensitivity in each mode and no more than 1/100 clean false positives. No unknown LP2 image has been classified. See [#736](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/issues/736), [grayscale SFT](../sims/f5_detector_calibration/gray_roundtrip_sft.go), [274-D gate](../sims/f5_detector_calibration/blind274_gate.py), [pilot run](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43412746381) and [full validation run](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43413416787).

3. **Recoverable missing historical input.** The solved end text points to an unresolved 64-byte hash target. Bounded searches of archived Cicada-stage assets and documented candidate encodings are preferable to unconstrained guessed URLs. A verified digest match needs exact bytes and a specified algorithm before anyone infers an LP2 key. A completed original-input test of the historically proposed **plain IPv4 dotted-quad preimage** exhaustively hashed all **2³²** canonical ASCII addresses against the p56 target under both original BLAKE-512 and SHA-512, with **zero matches** in either full search (32 successful matrix jobs, 8,589,934,592 total hashes). This retires those two finite preimage hypotheses without identifying the actual algorithm, missing page or other input format ([complete reproducible decision](../im-results/anend-ipv4-historical-512-full-search.md); [BLAKE run](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43164174106); [SHA run](https://github.com/FlareZ123/liber-primus/actions/runs/43164895409)). The 2014 Stage11 solver-archive commit `06f57643821f1cadf597471c2b9c813bd08d2e37` also contains a 67-byte README saying `59.txt` contains text from page 59 and prescribing `xxd -r -p 59.txt > 59.bin`; this is not a Cicada-clearsigned instruction. Neither 59 object exists in that commit or the original repository history. A census of all 21 GitHub forks found no copy: each shares the pinned `56.txt` blob as a positive control but lacks both 59 siblings. The preserved `.onion.to` CDX snapshot lists only the root and `robots.txt`. A fresh 2026-11-11 live CDX probe returned 14 Onion7 `.onion.to` records but only that root/robots content; six other documented proxy suffixes returned zero and four remained inconclusive because of timeouts/connection failures. The broader wildcard query hit Internet Archive 503s, so no wildcard negative is claimed. The p56 “within the deep web” instruction followed by p57 “instar tunneling to the surface” may point to a Tor2web-style gateway; this is a human-solve-path hypothesis, not an authenticated address. Fixed-output seven-host and Common Crawl probe artifacts were produced, but the Wayback positive control returned HTTP 503 and both Common Crawl positive controls returned HTTP 504; no Cicada-host queries ran, so no archive negative is established. The page-59 bytes remain unrecovered; this is a concrete missing-input lead, not proof of a Cicada-authenticated page or hash algorithm. A bounded p55 output-contract review finds no source-selected format: p56 asks for a separate page that hashes to the printed target, while p57's instar parable gives no address grammar. The 2014 59.txt README is an unsigned participant note with both referenced files absent. The p55 FOLD-derived THE DEEP END phrase is a post-selected, non-unique navigation candidate, not p55 plaintext or a proven locator; literal p57 word-shape reuse is rejected. Therefore #41 should require complete 76-rune plaintext without imposing a Tor-address or hash-preimage format. See [the p55 output-contract decision](../im-results/archive-hash-preimage.md#p55-output-contract-decision-from-p56p57-and-stage11-director-490). The bounded p19 OutGuess passphrase set `{19,133,331,361}` (image link `19.jpg`, archived HTML title `133`, div id `331`, and the p57 numerical association `[361,133,331]`) was tested on original scan `36.jpg`: passwords 19/133/331 yield high-entropy embedded tails with no recognized magic or valid OpenPGP packet; 361 fails the iterator. Neither whole outputs nor embedded tails match the p56 SHA-512 target. The exact candidate hashes and corrected extractor metadata are in [the p19 source gate](../im-results/cicada2014-signed19-onion7-title133-source-gate.md). The solved p57 plaintext file itself hashes to `0dc6be584f9e27ba73ae018054b45e5daa351d17510e512ec3100f81877296448a987a575ecea5845deecebeaf9fb6e09b4051b6a17dbaa1472e31d1f6449d99` (LF) and `d436e901c28a2713e2abd2741b13ad29170e28f2851544ca9057b6ab5e8b8e111294962346ae4fb13b1d769e3109cfe50727918d609be1528bc16bff3143a168` (no final LF), not the target. These exact-byte exclusions do not prove the missing page is unavailable. ([bounded source census](../im-results/archive-hash-preimage.md#stage11-59txt-59bin-bounded-repository-and-archive-census-11-november-2026)). The once-unretrievable 2016 Pastebin URL `http://pastebin.com/uRkNsGsa` is now attested by a **7 January 2016 byte-verified IRC archive**: a participant labelled it a convenient copy of the already-known hash, and a contemporaneous preview titled it “Liber Primus p.57 hash.” This source-backed echo does not identify the missing target page or give a hash preimage. The separate 2019 answer naming `dxwd42hgpd7qrccm.onion`, `1711141131131.xyz` and `cicada_317.mp4` has no reproducible preimage: 13 literal candidates miss all five tested 512-bit families, the source-derived 153-byte BASE29 integer from the exact p50 dual-factor quotient also misses the p56 target under the same five families (0/5; conditional on base60; see [hash-preimage report](../im-results/archive-hash-preimage.md)), the 2017 archive manifest marks recovered assets not PGP-signed with attribution unverified, and independent third-party records place the domain in a work-in-progress `Sevens_Exposed` branch and the MP4 in a 2019 Premiere project. The 2017 root response is now byte-preserved as a **7,063-byte Wayback `id_` entity**. Its Memento date, source-WARC identifier, original content length, and SHA-1 base32 digest match the CDX record exactly; body SHA-256 is `2bcfba6bbd27738ba3bab0033713d4fc533be8f1a26b5b55de3d7274c79c590b` ([exact entity, headers and metadata](../resources/external/warc/1711141131131.xyz/20170629-root/README.md); [full assessment](../im-results/archive-hash-preimage.md#2017-archivebot-root-html-exact-wayback-id_-replay-recovered-agent3-11-november-2026)). The page title is “Elsa ~ 628”; it contains a poem, a large decimal block, and media references, but no PGP armour marker; the media was not fetched. This establishes what the 2017 site displayed, **not Cicada authorship**. Treat it as an **unauthenticated third-party side branch**, not a verified Cicada continuation or p56 destination. The original paste bytes remain uncaptured, and the previous ten URL/filename Whirlpool checks are bounded negatives only. The newly source-mapped 2014 LP1 OutGuess 00–02 share bodies (991-byte raw forms and 1,982-character lowercase-hex forms) and their 991-byte XOR-reconstructed PGP clear-signed message were also checked as exact SHA-512 candidates; none matches. This does not exclude an unarchived fifth-Onion page identified by that source chain ([original IRC source audit](../im-results/2016-pastebin-irc-original-source-audit.md); [updated hash assessment](../im-results/archive-hash-preimage.md); [SFT](../sims/anend_whirlpool_pastebin_hint_gate.py)). Keep the hash algorithm, canonical preimage and destination unresolved.

4. **Alternative source language or segmentation only with a predictive decoder.** Low word-order dependence, global adjacent-repeat suppression across both within-word and interword transitions (with a similar rate at printed-line boundaries), and genuine author-vocabulary mismatch make the plaintext register an open variable. Specify a reversible symbol/word-boundary map and test it on untouched original material rather than maximizing a dictionary score on the same source-selected patterns.

**Promotion rule:** a new unknown plaintext claim needs original ciphertext offsets, an explicit alphabet, an independently justified algorithm and key/state, a complete round-trip encoder/decoder, untrained original-page predictions, and documented failure controls. Preserve negative results under `im-results/` with their exact finite scope. Promote robust general findings here only when they materially change the cryptanalytic frontier.
